Nārada Muni, “the transcendental spacemen,” always travels to all the universes and, accompanied by his *vīṇā,* glorifies the holy name of Lord Nārāyaṇa. So, similarly, your success in spreading Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy name around the whole world leads us to suspect that Nārada Muni may have manifested himself through your personality.

At an age when people usually retire, you very enthusiastically preached Kṛṣṇa consciousness in many countries around the world and continually wrote books that destroy the nescience of the Age of Kali.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, we can feel your wonderful influence nourishing the spirituality of the world. Physically you are not present now, but your influence can be increasingly felt as your teachings continue to be spread through distribution of your books and through other means. As Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura wrote on the tomb of Haridāsa Ṭhākura:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas dies to live and, living,  
Try to spread the holy name around!

Empowered by the Supreme Lord, you have published many books to help us in our spiritual life in this Kali-yuga. You know all about the bad qualities of Kali-yuga, such as ignorance, so without your books how could we become enlightened? That’s why you always stressed the publication and distribution of your books. You humbly said, “If you love me, please distribute my books.”

In addition to your books, you built a house in which the whole world can live: ISKCON. ISKCON is your body, you said, and since your body is pure, ISKCON is also pure. If one thinks that ISKCON is material, one can easily lose faith in ISKCON and eventually leave ISKCON. ISKCON is like a big boat in the middle of the ocean of *māyā* that is taking humanity to the world without suffering. And you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the captain of that boat.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, we believe you are a *śaktyāveśa*-*avatāra* for this age, delivering us from the illusion of ignorance. You have prepared everything for us: books, ISKCON, and your representatives, whom you have sent to every country. In Indonesia there are now several preaching centers and temples. Here in Bali we have the Śrī Śrī Nitāi-*Gaurāṅga* Ashram, where the presiding Deities are *Śrī* *Śrī* Nitāi-*Gaurāṅga*, *Śrī* *Śrī* Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, and *Śrī* *Śrī* Rādhā-Gopīnātha, the main Deities. All this is possible only by your causeless mercy.

On this auspicious day we beg that you always take care of us by sending your representatives to guide us so that we will be able to cooperate to increase our devotional service to Guru and Kṛṣṇa.

The devotees at the Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga temple in Baha, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Śrutadeva Dāsa)

## Bangkok

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

We feel most fortunate to have the chance to glorify you and all of your achievements. Most of all we thank you for bringing us to the path of devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. We know that we are not at all qualified but that it is all your causeless mercy. We pray that we can establish your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement here in Thailand.

You once gave some instructions regarding preaching here. Although you had planned to visit here, somehow your plans changed and you could not come. But in a letter dated 14 December 1974, you sent a message to the people of Bangkok. You wrote:

The Krishna Consciousness mission is not sectarian. We want to do what the United Nations has failed to do. The human form of body is an opportunity for solving all the problems of life. Through evolution we come to the human life for solving this problem. The real problem is the repetition of birth, death, disease, and old age.

But there is another status of life where there is no birth, death, disease, and old age. The Krishna Consciousness movement is the means for promoting the human being to that status. This is a great science, and it is elaborately explained in the Bhagavad-gita.

More instruction came in a room conversation at the French farm community in 1976, related in volume 3 of Hari Śauri Prabhu’s *Transcendental Diary:*

Prabhupāda said that formerly Thailand was known as Siam, which means “Śyāma,” a name for Kṛṣṇa. “So they are devotees originally,” he said. He also mentioned that they manufacture very nice *mūrtis* of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

Since Thailand is now predominantly Buddhist, Yogeśvara wondered whether they might object to someone preaching what they may construe to be Hinduism.

“Don’t establish Deity,” Prabhupāda told him. “Talk on philosophy.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are just now preparing to reprint your *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is* in Thai, and we pray that with these books we can attract many Thai people to take up Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We beg your blessings in this attempt. May we remain free of material desire so that we can properly represent you and the glorious tradition of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Begging to remain in your service,

The devotees at ISKCON Bangkok, Thailand.  
(written by Bhakti-vighna-vināśa Narasiṁha Mahārāja)

## Baroda

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this 115th anniversary of your appearance day, kindly accept our humble plea to remain your servants, in the service of your ISKCON.

ISKCON Baroda, just one tiny branch of the worldwide institution that you began forty-five years ago, is one of ISKCON India’s larger centers, a center where hundreds visit every day for the *darśana* of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmsundara, Śrī Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā Devī, and Śrī Śrī *Gaura*-Nitāi.

On our six-acre campus are Their Lordships’ temple, a *gośālā* with forty-five cows, calves and bulls, a Govinda’s restaurant, a Govinda’s snack bar, a gift shop, a twenty-five room guesthouse, and two large community halls for both in-house and rental functions.

ISKCON Baroda is famous in Baroda city for its two major annual festivals, Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā and Kṛṣṇa Janmāṣṭamī. We are happy to report that these festivals attract the participation of Baroda’s political leaders from both major parties, as well as tremendous local press coverage.

ISKCON Baroda celebrates most major Vaiṣṇava festivals with massive *prasādam* distribution, often feeding up to thirty thousand visitors. And every day after the *sandhyā* *ārati* we distribute *prasādam* to hundreds of members of the visiting public, in front of the temple.

ISKCON Baroda has a very active congregational and youth preaching program, and a number of newcomers regularly attend lectures, temple festivals, and even the daily temple functions. They do regular *harināma* *saṅkīrtana* and book distribution at various places in the city.

We know that we are far from perfect and can improve the temple campus in several ways. We are planning to continue to gradually increase the beauty and functionality of the six-acre campus, which you have mercifully given us to manage.

Your humble servants here pray that you continue to bless our humble efforts from your abode in the spiritual world, so that ISKCON Baroda can increase the success that we have experienced in the eleven years since the inauguration of the temple on the auspicious day of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Janmāṣṭamī, 1999.

With the utmost humility and gratefulness for the service you have so mercifully given us, we remain

Your servants at ISKCON Baroda, India.  
(written by Bāsu Ghosh Dāsa)

## Bologna

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet, which are still traveling throughout this world in the form of your books, saving it from the tidal wave of the Kali-yuga.

In this remote little place we are trying to spread your teachings through your books. We are taxing our tiny brains, trying to find novel ways to attract new people to our little preaching center, called Centro Vaikuntha. The people here are suffering so much anxiety due to the ever-increasing pressures of Kali-yuga, and we are sure that if they come in contact with you they will experience the Vaikuṇṭha atmosphere and become free from anxiety.

This city is home to the oldest university in the Western world, and therefore it has a great number of atheists and speculators. So it is a good field to conquer. In March of 1972 you wrote to me when I lived in the temple in Berkeley, a university town much like Bologna:

That is one of our most important branches, especially because it is located just in a very large university area, well known in your country as a famous seat of learning. So now it will be very important to flood Berkeley city with our books and literatures, because if we can convince the intelligent class of men of our Krishna philosophy, then our success for changing the position of your country and the rest of the world from a very dangerous condition is assured.

You once called the modern schools slaughterhouses, and here we can see how true that is. The students’ spiritual nature is being systematically destroyed; therefore they fall to the most degraded materialistic life. You gave the solution for this most fallen student class in another letter to me concerning preaching in Berkeley:

I am pleased to know that all of you are enthusiastically engaged in distributing our books. In this modern Godless civilization, everyone is greatly suffering due to lack of the real knowledge of Krishna consciousness, and our task is very great and very important. We must deliver this pure science of God to everyone and give them the chance to make their lives successful. \[5 May 1972\]

Then, even though I was so uneducated, you sent me to another university city—Boston—and wrote to me again, showing how concerned you were with recruiting some good men from the educated class. They are not easy to deal with, but they have the greatest potential to do something for this movement. You wrote:

Recruit some educated men from the colleges, schools in Boston, train them up for preaching mission, in this way develop the quality of your men to be the most surrendered servants of Krsna. That will please Krsna, and you will feel also yourself becoming more and more happy in perfecting your life. \[27 December 1972\]

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Kṛṣṇa will be pleased only if you are pleased. You gave us clear directions on how to please you: to give up all personal considerations and give our life and soul to carrying on this mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In addition to book distribution, we do a weekly *harināma* right through the university area, when all the students are out after the week’s classes are over. Our one devotee who was once a student here is in his turn finding novel ways to introduce Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the students. We hope the little efforts of all the devotees here please you in some way, since that is the only goal of our lives—to see you smile on us.

Your servants at the ISKCON yātrā in Bologna, Italy.  
(written by Trai Dāsa)

## Brahmapur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy the devotees of ISKCON Brahmapur made you a fine new wooden *vyāsāsana,* with a peacock theme. Visiting devotees find your *vyāsāsana* very pleasing and also feel your physical presence in this temple. Indeed, here you are the center of attraction for everyone.

Our main temple construction work has reached up to the first floor, one floor above ground level. For the second floor up to roof level, all the pillars are ready. This year we hope to finish all concrete construction work up to the dome level. Then, step by step, we will try to complete the interior, by your mercy.

Since your great desire was that we distribute as many of your books as possible, we are trying our best to distribute more and more of your books to the general public and life members.

We, the temple devotees, congregational devotees, and well-wishers in Brahmapur, pray at your lotus feet on this 115th anniversary of your auspicious appearance day. Please shower your mercy on us so that we can finish our temple project as soon as possible.

The devotees of ISKCON Brahmapur, Orissa, India.

## Brampton

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Where do we begin? How can we ever show our complete gratitude? Why have you been so merciful to us all? We can never truly comprehend the answers to these questions, and we can also never fully reciprocate all that you have given us. We’ve been so blessed to have you and your legacy around us in so many ways—from your books, to your disciples, to your philosophy, to your establishments, and mostly to the imprints that you have left in society right up until today. Hope for a better, God-conscious future seems possible when we think of you, and achievable with your guidance. Our desire to go back home, back to Godhead, has arisen only by your mercy. Your courage, along with your passion to serve Lord Caitanya and His mission, has left an impression in so many ways and has taught us to be fearless as we pick up the armor of the holy name and use it to defend against the illusionary energy of this material world.

You did this all not only to serve your guru, going above and beyond an ordinary person’s capacities, but also to help us mere mortals who were clueless until we caught a glimpse of your energy and efforts.

Our congregation has had the good fortune of being established near a city, Toronto, where you once left your mark and which has become one of the main hubs for Kṛṣṇa consciousness in North America. Some of your great followers in Toronto have brought together devotees in the suburb where we dwell and helped us form another stand-alone Kṛṣṇa conscious center.

From the days of traveling a long distance to the only temple in the Toronto area, to renting a nearby gymnasium one morning a week, to eventually being able to lease a building we now call the Bhaktivedanta Cultural Centre—none of this would have been possible if you were not the shining example you are. A good portion of our congregation consists of couples investing in their children’s future by having them attend our Sunday school, which is based on the Vedic educational philosophy you set out for us. The success of our school has generated attendance numbers that are already starting to surpass our immediate adult congregation. Just as you’ve taught us, we see that our children are the future of this movement, and their determination and hard work have forced us to raise our own devotional standards so as to ensure that we are doing a diligent job as leaders and, more importantly, servants.

Through attrition, we have felt the challenge of accepting the fact that some of our senior members, who were the core of our congregation and who helped pioneer what you represented, are moving on to bigger projects to help spread the holy name. For those of us who were spiritually guided by them, their absence has forced us to raise our own devotional standards.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, not only have you left us your magnificent institution, ISKCON, but you are currently teaching us to grow and accept our responsibility, as you beautifully explain in your *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is*. Not only are we trying to continue to serve you and carry forward all that you demonstrated, but we want to serve all those who have served you and granted us the opportunities we now have to perform devotional service. Our congregation has grown and matured devotionally and really begun to identify themselves as servants of the servant. Now that we have established ourselves as one of your many ISKCON centers in the world, we are attempting to take what you’ve taught us out to others in any way we can. Devotees have stepped up to ensure that we are not only preserving our Sunday program but also taking care of our congregation as best as we can. They are adamant to serve you to the best of their abilities, and our devotional services team is making sure that not only are we well prepared for Sunday’s and festivals but that we are also utilizing our Fridays and eventually other days of the week so that we are all well prepared and inspired to perform some form of *saṅkīrtana* in the region of Peel. The desire of the congregation is pushing us to go beyond the perimeter of the temple, and their sincerity is lifting us above the walls that house our Deities, *Śrī* *Śrī* Rādhā-Dāmodara. Now all we need is the dedication, determination, and enthusiasm you demonstrated—and, of course, your blessings and mercy.

We beg at your lotus feet that the streets of Brampton, Mississauga, Oakville, Burlington, and eventually Milton can hear the joyous chanting of the *mahā-mantra* and get a glimpse of your army, whose mission it is to take us all out of our misery by chanting:

Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/  
Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for connecting us to Lord Kṛṣṇa and inspiring us to connect others to Him also. Even though most of our congregation never had the opportunity to meet you when you were on this earth, your name, your books, and your devotees make you more tangible than we ever thought possible.

We can never repay the debt we owe you for saving us from a bleak, miserable existence and giving us the opportunity to purify ourselves—in body, mind, and soul. We pray that we may remain forever at the shelter of your lotus feet. Please continue to guide us, and bless us so that we may grow stronger and stronger in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and spread the holy name of Kṛṣṇa further and wider, as you wanted us to.

Your congregation at ISKCON Brampton, Canada.

## Brisbane

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you on this most auspicious day of your appearance.

Year after year we hear the story of how you spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the whole world. But it seems that as time passes and we mature, the story only becomes more astonishing. We grow older and often seek comfort and solace in these times, but in 1966, at the age of seventy, you wrote:

I am here alone without any devotee companion. But I have come here to serve the Lord and not for personal happiness. I am prepared to live in hell even if I am able to serve the Lord. Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu wanted that this mission should be propagated all over the world and that is my objective. I do not mind the inconvenience personally felt. ACB

For this statement alone, we owe you lifetimes of gratitude and service. Lord Caitanya mercifully gave *bhakti,* the highest, to the people of this fallen age, the lowest. But it was you specifically who delivered His mercy to us. Your actions are unique and unparalleled in the history of the world. Not only did you come for us, fearless and fully surrendered, but you were so kind and had such attractive personal qualities that it was easy to become attached to you and serve you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our greatest hero. We offer you our humblest obeisances, and our hearts.

Your servants at ISKCON Brisbane, Australia.

## Canberra

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Today is Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, the day set aside for honoring the representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva. The representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva sits on the *vyāsāsana,* the seat of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, and is part of an unbroken disciplic succession. The spiritual master, having heard and realized the transcendental message, and acting under the direction of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, delivers the fallen souls from the network of *māyā*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are your followers at ISKCON Canberra, Australia, and although our temple is quite small we are trying to use to the best advantage the facilities that Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa has provided.We have nine thousand square meters of land in a prime location in Canberra, and we are planning to build a new community center and temple there in the future.

We are trying to follow your teachings on how to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, how to reactivate that which we’ve lost due to our contact with *māyā*. We are holding festivals and coming together in a spirit of co-operation, trying to create a spiritual atmosphere of *sādhu*-*saṅga* to carry on the mission of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu. We all feel the beneficial effects of chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* and our understanding of what is matter and what is spirit is deepening as transcendental knowledge is revealed to us. Of course, we are in our infancy as devotees and are struggling to become free from *māyā,* but this struggle is the greatest thing that a human being can do.

Previously, before coming in contact with you, we did not know this. We were not aware of the value of the human form of life—we were not taking advantage of this gift. We were lost as in a dream, thinking illusion to be reality.

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we owe our lives to you alone. We owe everything to you for the bounty we have received.

Thank you for going to America on the *Jaladuta,* thank you for your determination and for your vision of how to reach those who had never heard of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you for Ratha-yātrā, thank you for the Deity of the Lord, thank you for your books, thank you for the recordings of your *bhajans,* and thank you for devotee association. And most of all, thank you for the holy name.

On this great occasion, our collective prayer is to always be able to remember you, the one who gave us Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Canberra, Australia.  
(written by Svayambhur Dāsa)

## Cape Town

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

It is not easy for ordinary conditioned living entities such as ourselves to estimate your glories, but we welcome the opportunity to make this offering to you based on whatever little realization we may have. We consider this a wonderful opportunity for our purification.

While thinking of your glories, we decided to take help from Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja *Gosvāmī*. In the second chapter of the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta’s Antya*-*līlā,* he informs us that Lord Kṛṣṇa, in the form of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, descended to this world to deliver all the living entities of this universe. He further describes that He caused their deliverance in three ways: He delivered the fallen souls in some places by meeting them directly, in other places by empowering a pure devotee, and in still other places by appearing before someone Himself. Later in the chapter, Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Goswami says: “To deliver the people in regions throughout the universe who could not meet Him, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu personally entered the bodies of pure devotees.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it seems to us that Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has entered your body. Your monumental achievements prove it. Specifically, you have achieved what even Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself did not do. Lord Caitanya spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement all over India while He was present, but you have fulfilled His own prediction that His name would be chanted in every town and village all over the world. From the fact that Lord Caitanya reserved this glory for you, we can understand how dear you must be to Him. This, however, is not the full extent of your glory.

It is said that a man’s greatness is measured not by his achievements while present in this world but by his influence once he has departed. By this definition, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your true glory becomes clearly visible. Even after your departure from this world, you continue to influence the souls of Kali-yuga to take up the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and purify their lives. This you achieve through the agency of your books, and it is truly miraculous. But there is more. Your glorious ISKCON society provides the environment where everyone can come to gain shelter from the blazing fire of Kali-yuga, to benefit from the association of devotees and make spiritual progress. ISKCON is your *āśrama,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, and your *āśrama* extends all over the world. Therefore you have been designated *jagat-guru,* the guru for the whole world.

In the *āśrama* of the guru, he is naturally the most prominent personality. This is the proper understanding in ISKCON also. Therefore you insisted on the title founder-*ācārya*. Not to gain personal prominence, but to protect your *āśrama* after your departure. Also, in the *āśrama* of the guru everyone naturally has a close relationship with him. In ISKCON this is also the case, for those that want it. It is a mistake to think that it is impossible to have a close relationship with you today because you are no longer physically present. You are available in many ways. You yourself said, “If you want to know me, read my books.” You are also very much present in your *mūrti* form, watching over us like a loving father. Another wonderful way to have a close relationship with you is through your followers. All our own spiritual fathers, along with our spiritual uncles, bring your presence so close through their remembrances and realizations. They amplify your presence and illuminate your moment-by-moment influence and guidance. They allow us to come close to you in ways that would otherwise be impossible.

Another way that we can understand your designation as *jagat-guru* is that in a broad historical sense you are the current *ācārya* who guides the destiny of the community of Vaiṣṇavas. An *ācārya* is understood as one who teaches not only by instruction but by his own personal example. Therefore the term *ācārya* is used to indicate a bona-fide spiritual master—a learned teacher of the spiritual science. But in the highest sense, the *ācārya* is the soul sent by Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself to teach the whole world. This is the truest sense of the term as it is applied to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All those who wish to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the most perfect way must validate their realizations and conclusions in reference to your teachings.

By careful analysis of your life and teachings, we cannot but come to the conclusion that Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has personally empowered you to reveal in the clearest and best way the Lord’s desire for this world. Also that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, embody most perfectly the Supreme Lord’s mission in this world in the current age.

These statements represent our broad understanding of your glories, but on a deeply personal level there are so many things that we want to say to you. We want to thank you so much for revealing our own spiritual fathers to us. We have no doubt that this is completely your mercy. We feel so blessed by our glorious spiritual fathers and so many spiritual uncles. We also feel blessed that you are easily and so personally available in your books, in your *mūrti* form, and through your followers. We feel so blessed to be part of your ISKCON, your *āśrama*.

It is true that so many blessings are there when one is a member of your ISKCON *āśrama*. It is also true that for *āśrama* members there are responsibilities. It seems to us that the responsibility we have to you, our own spiritual masters, our spiritual uncles, and the previous *ācāryas* is essentially quite simple. Namely, that having realized something of your glories and the incredible mercy you are offering us, we must try to connect others to that mercy. We must become your goodwill ambassadors, Śrīla Prabhupāda, “transparent via media” to you. We know that no one can spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness without the mercy of Lord Caitanya. We also know that you are undoubtedly very close to Him. Therefore, we are hoping that you will kindly ask Him if we might be allowed to do something to spread His mission.

Your servants at ISKCON Cape Town, South Africa.

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Once again we have the opportunity to directly glorify Your Divine Grace. Though the opportunity to glorify you is there at every moment if we live by your guidelines, still, being able to write of your oceanic qualities in a Vyāsa-pūjā homage is special.

Recently His Holiness Bhakti Cāru Swami, one of your wonderful disciples, visited Chandigarh. He emphasized that ISKCON was founded by you not to last for a few years but for ten thousand years. Therefore the teachings in your books are meant to be applied not only to our lives but to the lives of many, many generations of devotees to come. This is one reason why His Holiness Gopāl Krishna Goswami, our GBC, pushes us to increase book distribution every year. In the 2010 marathon we achieved the highest score in the history of our Chandigarh center, distributing over 26,000 copies of *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is,* all by your blessings and the enthusiastic encouragement of Gopāl Krishna Mahārāja.

Sometimes we cannot understand the importance of book distribution, but as we read your purports more and more, we understand the value of your books more and more. The message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* explained in your purports, is clear and consistent, i.e., to render uninterrupted loving devotional service unto the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa without any expectation of profit, material or spiritual. Just to quote a few examples:

The supreme occupation \[dharma\] for all humanity is that by which men can attain to loving devotional service unto the transcendent Lord. Such devotional service must be unmotivated and uninterrupted to completely satisfy the self. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.2.6, translation\]

The real perfection of life is therefore to be engaged in the transcendental loving service of the Lord. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 2.7.48, purport\]

In pure devotional service, the only motive should be to please the Supreme Personality of Godhead. That is not actually a motive; that is the pure condition of the living entity. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.29.8, purport\]

Another senior devotee who recently visited our center explained that it is only by your mercy that the understanding of Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead is so clear and so easily available to us. As seen from the few quotations above, you always focus on the goal, i.e., rendering pure devotional service, without compromise. As the saying goes, “You can lead others to where you yourself are prepared to go.” Since you are an unflinching servitor of your spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa, you can carry the whole world on the wave of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Otherwise, even in India, in the name of so-called Hinduism, so much hodge-podge goes on. There is no clarity, no focus. Some give importance to various demigods, lumping in Viṣṇu and Kṛṣṇa among them, and others get absorbed in performing rituals for some material return. But hardly anyone is teaching the concept of loving devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. Many advocate vegetarianism, cow protection, preserving nature, etc., but where is the stress on pure love for Kṛṣṇa alone? It is only by your causeless mercy that we have access to this divine knowledge. You have meticulously chalked out our path, by choosing very carefully what is to be given and what is not.

Although you were busy with so many services—spreading the movement far and wide, managing the Society, managing individual temple projects, dealing with devotees’ personal issues, responding to challenges from governments and other sources—still you always gave priority to the dictation of your Bhaktivedanta purports, since this was the direct instruction from your spiritual master, Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. Knowing the importance of these books, you sacrificed your sleep and just dictated your Bhaktivedanta purports.

O dear Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please shower your causeless mercy on us so that we can also bathe in the nectar of your purports, relish them, digest them, apply them in our lives, and distribute them to others.

Congregational preaching is increasing in our center, with regular structured courses. Also, devotees are getting strength from senior devotees who visit. A Bhakti Śāstrī course is being conducted by devotees from the Māyāpur Institute of Higher Education (MIHE). Forty devotees, including temple *brahmacārīs* and congregational members, are taking advantage of this highly enlivening spiritual course. This is our humble attempt to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, since you always wanted your followers to be well versed in the *śāstras*.

Youth preaching in the temple and at colleges is gradually expanding, and we have many young devotees practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness under your shelter. We are also catering to the spiritual needs of young girls and small children through the IYF girls program and the Bhakta Prahlad School, respectively.

On this divine day of your appearance, we beg your blessings so we may fulfill your mission successfully. The blessings of the spiritual master cure the disciple of the disease of ignorance and lethargy and empower him to preach. Please bless us so that we can understand your teachings in truth and become useful instruments in your hands to spread the mission of your spiritual master.

Yours servants at ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

## Czech Republic

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

There is nothing new to be said. Everything is in my books. Therefore, if you want to know me, read my books.

So there is nothing new to be said. Whatever I have to speak, I have spoken in my books. Now you try to understand it and continue your endeavor. Whether I am present or not present, it doesn’t matter.

My books will be the law books for the next ten thousand years

These quotes resound deeply in our hearts, enlivening us with the certainty that we will always have Your Divine Grace’s association as long we read, study, contemplate, and accept everything you said, without the slightest doubt, second thought, or hesitation.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your purports are clear, straightforward, and unequivocal. The same points are repeated again and again, and are further confirmed in your lectures and your conversations with disciples and guests. You explain every principle from all sides, with numerous examples. Thus the unique philosophy of the soul’s relationship with the Supreme Lord is expounded by His dearmost servant, following in the footsteps of the previous *ācāryas*.

Increasing faith in your words compels us to please you and to surrender our life to your desire.

There is no greater pleasure then discussing your plans, visions, ideas, and instructions and in this way discovering how they apply to every aspect of our lives. There is no grater pleasure than reading your words glorifying Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. Your books are alive, filled with transcendental potency, always available as our best friend. Living with your books, we are never alone, for we have the best possible association in the three worlds.

We are confident that imbibing from Your Divine Grace your knowledge, values, principles, and mood of complete surrender will place us under the shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord.

Please bless us that we never forget that success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness means becoming completely dependent on your mercy so that we can become fully dedicated devotees wherever we are. Please give us the intelligence to think, decide, and act as you would, leaving aside our own ideas and plans born of ignorance.

Let us never forget your words but rather keep them always alive and fresh in our hearts. In this way we will repeat them with full realization for the benefit of the upcoming generations of your students.

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Navadvīpacandra Mandir, Czech Republic.  
*(*written by Narākriti Dāsa)

## Debrecen

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you.

Today is your Vyāsa-pūjā, celebrated every year by your devotees all over the world. Although most devotees here in Hungary know you only through the teachings you have given in your books and through the memories of your disciples, these devotees have been serving you with wonderful enthusiasm since the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement began here. At this time of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we express our gratitude to you for the chance you gave us to perfect our lives.

You traveled on the *Jaladuta* in very difficult conditions just to bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the West. During the twelve years you preached in the Western countries, you carefully trained your disciples, some of whom are now able to lead us in spiritual life. Just to look after us, you arranged for your grandchildren to be guided by your disciples just as your disciples were personally guided by you.

Our guides follow you systematically, consistently, and unwaveringly, and they don’t do anything else. They don’t want to change the process; they don’t want to add to it or take anything away from it. Just as you translated the *Bhagavad-gītā* “as it is,” they follow the devotional process “as it is,” teaching us by their own simple and pure example. And from this simplicity and purity they get unbelievable spiritual strength. Our greatest good fortune is that one of your followers came to Hungary and, out of his mercy, gave us the opportunity to join this wonderful spiritual movement. If we put faith in and follow his advice and do what he asks, we can also go home, back to Godhead.

We try to follow you through our authorities here in Debrecen. We understand that Kṛṣṇa consciousness can spread if devotees who do not live in a temple show that it is possible to lead an exemplary family life and be Kṛṣṇa conscious at the same time. Therefore we founded a little cultural center where we can practice and preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and we also began a restaurant that provides *prasādam* for the fallen souls. We just try to do our duty, and because the congregational devotees conjointly founded and now support a preaching center and a restaurant, we have become an example in the Hungarian *yātrā*. To tell the truth, we don’t really understand why it works, but we experience that Kṛṣṇa reciprocates with our tiniest attempt to follow your follower, to do what he asks, and we get everything we need for our preaching.

Whatever we have now is all due to your love for the fallen souls and your sincere desire to save us through Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please give us your mercy so we can hear and follow your followers and develop our service.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your fallen servants in Debrecen, Hungary.

## Denpasar

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to *you*!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, late in life, when most men stay home with their family, you set out to fulfill the order of your spiritual master to spread the knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Thus you spent your life in the best way.

In the *Caitanya-bhāgavata* Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted that “In as many towns and villages as there are on the surface of the earth, My holy name will be preached.” Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura predicted that there would appear a great personality who would spread the teachings of Lord *Caitanya* in the West. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you fulfilled that prediction, spreading *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* all over the world.

You built a house in which the entire world can live in peace. You are the true spiritual master for this universe. A *śaktyāveśa* *avatāra*, you have been empowered to save the human race. By reading your transcended books, millions of people have come to understand their real constitutional position and have gotten answers to life’s most important questions: “Who am I?” “Who is God?” “What is the goal of life?” By your oceanic mercy, the world is awakening from her long sleep.

Your books are truly a matchless gift, the guidebooks and lawbooks for the next ten thousand years.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

Your devotees in Denpasar, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Padma Locana Dāsa)

## Durban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace. All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha.

The devotees at your Rādhā-Rādhānātha Temple are always trying to fulfill your desires. You are so full of compassion and love for the conditioned souls that you tried to give every living entity an opportunity to be engaged in devotional service. Somehow, all of us serving here are trying in some little way to fulfill your desires. We would like to mention a few.

We are very blessed that you came to South Africa in 1975. During that visit you met Professor S. P. Olivier from the University of Durban Westville. In your lecture you stressed how the living entity is different from the body, and somehow, by your mercy, he seemed to understand this point. He expressed the view that there should be a detailed study of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* and when you asked him to allow a devotee to teach this spiritual subject matter at the university, he agreed. Our devotees had some discussions with the head of the religious studies department at the university, and we are happy to report that your *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is* has been used as a standard textbook over the years. Students from many different backgrounds have studied your *Bhagavad-gītā,* and we have received an amazing response from them. Since your visit, the preaching on this campus, as well as at other educational institutions, has increased tremendously. Devotees who study at these institutions have formed the Bhakti Yoga Society, which hosts eight programs weekly on six campuses around Durban. To cater to the students who reside in dorms on campus, two programs are held in the evening. Attractive festivals are held, such as music festivals featuring talented young devotees and food festivals featuring *prasādam*. Twice a hear we organize a retreat for students, where they have a chance to get a deeper insight into Kṛṣṇa consciousness and develop relationships with devotees. The success attained in preaching at the various educational institutions around the country is evidence of the importance of college preaching.

You once said that *Back to Godhead* magazine was the backbone of your Society. In this regard, it is noteworthy that the temple has a team of dedicated devotees responsible for the distribution of magazines. In the course of doing so, they have endeavored to bring new people to Kṛṣṇa consciousness (visitors to the temple, people we meet on the streets, friends and family, etc.) and engage them in devotional service. They have a list of approximately one hundred people who are engaged in the packing the BTG magazines. Many have started chanting the holy names, embracing the process of devotional service seriously.

Recently there have been many health fairs, well-being fairs, and astrology fairs in Durban. We have taken advantage of such opportunities to distribute books and magazines. These fairs are becoming very popular and attract a wide spectrum of society; we will increase our efforts to distribute books at such venues.

Our annual BTG Marathon and Durban Beachfront Festival of the Chariots are the yearly highlights for intense preaching and distribution of books and BTG magazines. Every year has seen an increase in distribution. In the course of the year, we arrange donor cultivation programs, in which we present a full Kṛṣṇa consciousness program (*kīrtana* and *ārati,* class, *prasādam*) where a significant number of donors live. At these programs, donors and their immediate families can experience intense Kṛṣṇa consciousness. These programs also allow us to personally interact with our donors, the result of which is sometimes increased monthly donations. The team also makes personal visits to donors’ homes to offer them *prasādam*. Donors are touched by this effort.

You began crying in Māyāpur when you noticed that children were beating off dogs with sticks to get remnants of *prasādam* devotees had left on their plates. You said, “Nobody within a ten-mile radius of our temple should go hungry.” Our devotees are trying to the best of their abilities to apply that principle here and serve *prasādam* without discrimination. Our recent *prasādam* distribution at the various campuses has attracted many students to enquire about the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It is clearly evident that *prasādam* is one of your important weapons to attract people to the process.

Your Bhaktivedanta College of Education and Culture has been expanding over the years, and I’m sure you’ll be happy to learn that through the efforts of the College many academics and professionals are studying your books. Prisoners have also enrolled in courses at your College through the mail. It is indeed inspiring to see that they painstakingly complete their assignments. Recently a prisoner who had seriously taken to the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and been released began serving at your BBT House in Johannesburg.

To help your institution function in a smooth and efficient manner, our Rādhā-Rādhānātha Temple management has organized strategic planning meetings. Devotees with various abilities were chosen to participate in these meetings, and it is pleasing to report meaningful progress. As you said, organization and intelligence are important factors for your movement to continue.

On this auspicious day we all beg for your mercy and pray that you give us the intelligence necessary to execute devotional service purely.

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Your servants at the Rādhā-Rādhānātha Temple, Durban, South Africa.  
(written by Raghunātha Bhaṭṭa Dāsa)

## East Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Our most humble obeisances to you on this auspicious day of your appearance.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day, as we do every year, we reflect on so many of your wonderful pastimes. The devotees who had your personal association here on earth are getting fewer, and hearing the nectar from them is becoming even more precious. Here in East Hartford we have been most fortunate, with visits from so many of your disciples, each one of them inspiring so many in our congregation to serve Kṛṣṇa and hear more and more about Him.

This year several of your *bhakta-vṛnda* who have been inspired by your teachings through the years had the good fortune of receiving initiation from your beloved Nirañjana Swami. Everyone was ecstatic, and the initiations were witnessed by a large gathering of devotees joyful for the new initiates. Your mercy is so widespread that it sweeps even the most unfortunate souls into the good fortune of your association. Receiving help from your early disciples, listening to their stories about you, witnessing their examples, and associating with you through your books, we feel that you have captured our hearts, and we also feel that we are on our way home, back to Kṛṣṇa—that our search for what is true has ended. But if only it were so easy. At every turn we encounter *anarthas—*pride and false ego, material attachments and desires, foolish fears and lethargy—that slow our progress toward the goal. You and your disciples help put out the fires of *saṁsāra* that we encounter every day as we struggle on our way back home. We beg for your continuous mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and the mercy of your loyal followers.

So many of your servants here at East Hartford ISKCON are pushing forth to carry out your instructions of distributing your books using Smart Boxes. Even when we are not trying, your books have a way of distributing themselves. Such is your potency and the potency of your books. We know that you are still present here through your books, and we feel grateful that you continue to inspire us, even in our fallen condition.

Our most humble obeisances are unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to all your disciples and granddisciples who are carrying forth your mission and serving you with so much dedication. We can never repay our debt to you, or to them, for giving us this most valuable and permanent treasure of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda kī jaya!!

Your humble servants at ISKCON of East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.  
(written by Gītāmālā Devī Dāsī)

## Gainesville

Our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. On this auspicious day of your divine appearance, we submit this short poem composed for your pleasure.

**A Few Things You Taught Us**

The spirit drives the body as a person drives a car.  
Our bodies sicken, age, and die, however great we are.  
When we grow tired and expire, we’re not finished yet;  
Another body’s waiting, and who knows what kind we’ll get?  
Take all the rest you need, but not at dawn or just before;  
Eat all you can digest, and if you’re young, perhaps some more;  
Get married to a proper mate, if you desire sex;  
Protect yourself when needed, but in general, pay respects.  
Lamenting over what is done will only squander fuel;  
To hanker for some paradise consumes the lives of fools.  
The present day is all we have to cultivate success,  
and if we give this day to Kṛṣṇa, surely we’ll progress.  
Be sure to listen eagerly to someone more advanced  
and show compassion to those souls of lesser circumstance.  
And when you’re choosing friends, select some saintly devotees,  
for one becomes like one’s companions—that is guaranteed.  
The śāstras are our basis, and our force, integrity;  
utility’s our standard, and our essence, SKP.  
In transcendental sacrifice, all things are absolute.  
To nourish leaves and branches, pour the water on the root.  
Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa daily and your life will be sublime.  
A million dollars can’t buy back a moment of lost time.  
A Person far surpasses an impersonal supreme.  
Discussions of Lord Kṛṣṇa are the most enchanting themes.  
To decorate the Deity infuses one with bliss.  
A daily round of sādhana distills one’s consciousness.  
To cook for Kṛṣṇa’s pleasure makes a drudgery a joy,  
for serving Kṛṣṇa far surpasses merging in a void.  
A simple flower, sweetly offered, makes Lord Kṛṣṇa pleased.  
Do not delay to douse a fire, debt, or some disease.  
And should the Lord provide a pure devotee as your guide,  
the truths you’ll know, and debt you’ll owe, cannot be quantified.

Your servants at the Kṛṣṇa House, ISKCON Gainesville, Florida, USA.

## Gaura Hari Ashram

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to your holy lotus feet. Those who take shelter under your lotus feet are very fortunate in this material world.

On this very auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we would like to express our deepest gratitude for your causeless mercy upon us here at Gaura Hari Ashram in Gianyar, Bali, Indonesia. If you hadn’t left India to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, our lives would certainly have been spoiled. It is only by your mercy that we have gotten the great opportunity to chant the *mahā-mantra* and practice devotional service.

How wonderful it is that we can still feel your presence here through your disciples, our gurus who regularly visit and mercifully give us their association. How nice it is that we can get your association from your books and your audio and video recordings. Even though we are only disciples of your disciples, still, by your mercy we consider ourselves very lucky to have come in contact with this movement. You live forever in your books and your divine instructions.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in our Ashram we are trying to create a community for devotees. Fourteen devotee families now live nearby the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava Temple. We hope that by your mercy the number of devotees will increase manyfold. We hope that someday we will be able to establish a Vaiṣṇava village here. We are also trying to be self-sufficient in the matter of meeting the *bhoga* needs of our temple. By your mercy we have been able to plant paddy in a rice field near the temple, and we are also trying to plant some vegetables and some fruit trees on the land we have here.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are very insignificant. How can we describe your unlimited glories? It’s impossible, because the glories of a pure Vaiṣṇava like you are limitless. You have built a very wonderful home where the whole world can take shelter. In the history of the human race, you will always be glorified and remembered as the one whom the Lord sent to save us in our darkest time.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are very grateful for your causeless mercy. We beg your forgiveness for our offenses and mistakes. We pray at your holy lotus feet that we here in Gaura Hari Ashram can carry on your movement and that someday we will be able to establish a village based on *varṇāśrama*-*dharma*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī* *jaya*!

Your aspiring servants at Gaura Hari Ashram, Gianyar, Bali, Indonesia.

## Ghana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Let us offer our respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa in this world, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.

Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī*. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.

It is said that if one does not know to which port he is sailing, no wind is favorable. Similarly, worship of the Supreme without knowledge of the Supreme is unfavorable for the cultivation of *bhakti*. In the Western world there is so much worship of God without any knowledge of the Supreme Godhead. The consequence of such worship is atheism, impersonalism, and voidism.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your gifts to world are too numerous to enumerate, but your revelation to the world that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality Godhead stands supreme. How can we serve and love a Supreme Person we do not know? It is impossible to relate to a person we do not know!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your appearance on this day, in this world, is of great importance to the entire world. You have kindly appeared in order to reveal the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, to the confused people of this world. This you have done so expertly, so much so that even very fallen people like us can know and love the Supreme Lord just by chanting His holy names. On this most auspicious anniversary of your divine appearance, we pray that you kindly bless and empower your followers in Africa to embark on a massive distribution of your books, so that the African people may also come to know Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead and make their lives perfect.

*Your* devotees in Ghana.  
(written by Śrīvāsa Dāsa)

## Gītā-nāgarī

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances in the dust of your divine and sweet lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most blessed day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Although we have been given knowledge and understanding about Kṛṣṇa and the means to approach Him in disciplic succession for some time, we may take for granted what you have brought with you to this realm. The whole cosmic manifestation exists so that we may have access to spiritual guides who can teach us how to reform our consciousness and return to our original spiritual home, Goloka Vṛndāvana. The fact that you came to this world 115 years ago just to reclaim us is a miracle. And when you left Vṛndāvana in 1965 to come to New York City, that was truly an exhibition of Kṛṣṇa’s supreme mercy. How inconceivable it is that your mercy continues to deepen at every moment, making us more fortunate to have access to your grace.

It is said that of all Kṛṣṇa’s unlimited characteristics, His greatest is His compassion on the fallen souls. You are the personification of the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. This morning we read in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* that the fault of the conditioned souls is that we are all averse to the transcendental loving service of Kṛṣṇa.

Today we insignificant followers of yours pray to you that we may be freed from envy and become eligible to serve Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara again. As Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura quotes Lord Caitanya in his *Aruṇodaya* *Kīrtana:* “I have descended just to save you. Except for Me, who is your friend?” As Lord Caitanya’s representative, you are our actual friend because you are the one who entered the burning building of this material world to pull us out.

Here at *Gītā*-nāgarī we are endeavoring to maintain your farm project according to your desires. Many sincere devotees here hanker in their innermost heart to serve your mission of rescuing the fallen souls by tirelessly endeavoring to fulfill your request that we exemplify “simple living and high thinking.” There is much more that we can and will do in this regard, but in the meantime please accept our small attempts to serve and take care of your *Gītā*-nāgarī farm.

The images of your visit to *Gītā*-nāgarī in 1976, lovingly captured by your faithful disciples, remain a daily inspiration for us here. As we go about our daily services, we visualize you leisurely walking through the fields and grounds, inspecting everything to make sure it is the best it can be, given our conditioned natures and limited capacity. You encourage, tolerate, chastise, and never cease to be merciful. We carry in our hearts the sincere yearning to manifest your prophecy for *Gītā*-nāgarī, based on your instructions to us and your service to your spiritual master and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara. On this most auspicious day, we continue to pray that you will always bestow on us your protection, guidance, and love, and that we will always remain in your loving service, eternally.

*Cakhu-dān dilo jei, janme janme prabhu sei, divya jñān hṛde prokāśito*. You have opened our darkened eyes and filled our hearts with transcendental knowledge. You are our lord, birth after birth. We owe you everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your movement is simply wonderful, your followers are simply wonderful, and you are simply wonderful. We thank you and we love you with our life and soul. All glories to you, our dearmost, beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda!

We beg to remain your eternal and eager servants.

Your children, grandchildren, and great-grandchildren at the Gītā-nāgarī farm community in Port Royal, Pennsylvania, USA.

## Guyana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

None of us here in Guyana were lucky enough to have your personal association. Still, through your *vāṇī,* your *mūrti*, and your transcendental voice we feel very closely connected to you. Today I feel humbled, knowing well how unqualified to be asked to praise your glories on this special day, your Vyasa-puja. I am therefore begging you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please kindly accept this simple homage, our offering of love to you. We feel very confident that just as a loving grandfather feels pleased with the shoddy attempt of a grandson to please him, you may be satisfied with our meager endeavor to glorify you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have created our good fortune here in Guyana in the form of your ISKCON institution. More and more we are realizing how you have given our lives meaning, aim, and direction. We can never hope to repay you for your many, many invaluable transcendental gifts. If we really value these gifts, we will treasure them within the core of our hearts. You have left the greatest heritage: a house in which the whole world can find shelter. If we truly realize the value of your legacy, we will adjust our lives and do whatever it takes to maintain and expand your ISKCON movement.

We understand that your ISKCON movement is a direct manifestation of your compassion. Your ISKCON is complete, and because it was fashioned by your own hand, there cannot be any lack or flaw. If anyone sees inadequacy, it is because of their own defective vision and because of not applying themselves properly. By serving loyally within your ISKCON, we will automatically be blessed with the opportunity for spiritual enlightenment and purification, and our path back to Godhead will open.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel very obligated to you when we see how so many people are being transformed and becoming happy by taking to devotional service. Before you came to the West and founded ISKCON, we had never heard of Kṛṣṇa, but now, through your kindness, we are getting to know a bit about Him.

As an offering to you, we now have temples in all the counties in Guyana. We conduct three Ratha-yātrās a year, and we have also introduced a yearly *padayātrā*. Many of your senior sons visit our *yātrā* yearly to give us spiritual guidance and inspiration.

To show you how much we love you and how much we appreciate what you have given us, this year we are commissioning the construction of two new temples. The Nimai-Pandit Study Centre near the University of Guyana, probably our most important project, had its ground-breaking ceremony on Rāma-navamī 2011, marking the start of construction of a three-storey, 140-by-40-foot *mandira.* The second project is another temple, which has begun construction at Line Path, on the Corentyne.

We are all extremely grateful to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for everything you have given us. Therefore the least we can do is sincerely dedicate our lives to fulfilling your mission and pleasing you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, again we thank you for everything you have done for us—for saving us and so many others.

Your aspiring servants in Guyana.  
(written by Paramātmā Dāsa)

## Hong Kong

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I was born in ignorance, but mercifully my spiritual master has opened my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. You came to distribute love of Godhead to the most fallen. We are the most fallen, and therefore we are the most highly qualified to receive your mercy.**

*‘sādhu-saṅga’, ‘sādhu-saṅga’—sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

We are grateful to you for sending sādhus into our lives. Your beloved disciples continue to carry the compassion you exemplified, transforming our very lives and those of many others. We see this transformation vividly in this region, the Far East, where devotees are dedicating their lives to Kṛṣṇa consciousness out of love for you and your disciples. Some might have seen a devotee only once. Some might have just heard of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Some do not understand the language due to being from very remote parts of Khasadesh. Still, their love for Kṛṣṇa consciousness manifests in their smile, their dealings with devotees, and their service attitude toward Vaiṣṇavas. Some have been serving for countless years, dedicating their family life in the service of your mission. Some have taken on hardships to propagate your message by preaching in remote and unexplored parts of the world.

From the dedication of all these devotees to your mission, we can understand and experience your divine grace upon us. We beg for the gift of the twenty-five qualities of devotees so we can offer them in service to you. This will give us joy in our barren hearts and allowing us to experience Lord Kṛṣṇa’s protective shelter among the insecurities of modern life.

Please bless the humble steps we are taking to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to students in schools, colleges, and universities. We pray for the day when your name and the names of your beloved Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī* and Lord Kṛṣṇa will be heard and echoed in the halls of academia. Without your mercy, it is impossible for us to bring about the transformation of hearts in the upcoming generations of devotees.

We are forever grateful and indebted to you, our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the transformation of heart enabling us to offer this glorification to you.

Please forgive our foolish offenses and continue to guide us and shower your merciful blessings upon us. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Hong Kong.

## Karuna Bhavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, all glories to your ISKCON, all glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu.

Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is self-satisfied and does not really need our service. On the contrary, it is us who need to serve Kṛṣṇa, for by such service we are immensely benefited. Similarly, on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā ceremony, you do not really need our words of praise and glorification. On the contrary, we are purified by our attempts to remember and glorify you.

Kṛṣṇa is unlimited: His name is unlimited and His glories and qualities are unlimited. Ananta Śeṣa, who has unlimited mouths, has been glorifying Kṛṣṇa unlimitedly since time immemorial, yet He still has not reached the limits of those glories. Since you are Kṛṣṇa’s pure representative, your glories and qualities are unlimited like a multifaceted diamond. We can never reach the end of them. But still, for our own purification we shall try to describe some of those glories.

“Whatever action a great man performs, common people follow, and whatever standards he sets by exemplary acts, all the world pursues.” You set the perfect example in all respects: you taught us how to hear, how to chant, how to preach, how to dress, how to cook, how to clean, how to live, how to die, how to see everything in relation to Kṛṣṇa. You taught us how to engage everything and everyone in the Lord’s service. This is the meaning of *ācārya*—one who teaches by example. You did not instruct us to do anything that you were not prepared to do, indeed, that you were not doing yourself. The real leader is one who can follow; therefore you, being a perfect follower of the *guru*-*paramparā*, are the perfect leader.

At the time of your initiation, when your name came up as a candidate, you Guru Mahārāja said, “Yes, he likes to hear. I have marked him. I will accept him as my disciple.” You told us that you were always very enthusiastic to hear from your spiritual master, and that because of this enthusiasm for *śravaṇam* you became very enthusiastic about *kīrtanam*. By your *kīrtanam* you instilled faith in the faithless, you made atheists into lovers of God, you transformed an insatiable desire for material enjoyment into devotion to Kṛṣṇa, you changed ignorance into cognizance, you brought light where there was darkness, you transformed our futile lives into something of real meaning. You are still transforming our lives, and indeed the lives of countless souls.

He reasons ill who says that Vaiṣṇavas die,  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live and, living, try  
To spread the holy name around.

Therefore your presence is wholeheartedly felt by those who follow your teachings. You showed us that all success comes in spiritual life when one dedicates one’s life to following the order of the spiritual master. You also taught us that one should never take credit for oneself but should give all credit to guru and Kṛṣṇa, and in this humble state of mind one can chant the holy name constantly.

In one of your Bhaktivedanta Purports you state that becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious is more or less a declaration of war on *Māyā*. Just as *Māyā* inflicts suffering upon the living entities with her trident of the threefold miseries, so you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have given us the three-pronged attack on *Māyā* in the form of book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, and distribution of the holy name. This is the winning formula: people become purified by chanting the holy name and honoring *prasādam,* and then they become qualified to read your books. And by understanding your books, they perfect their lives.

You taught us to practice and preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness side by side. The only way to make spiritual advancement is by receiving your mercy (*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*). By receiving your mercy, we receive the mercy of Lord *Nityānanda* and Lord Caitanya, and only by Their mercy can we be delivered. You have shown us by your personal example that we should preach until our very last breath. You said that you wanted the benediction of dying on the battlefield of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You also told us that an ease-loving mentality and spiritual life go ill together. Therefore we pray to be able to follow in your footsteps and dedicate our lives to your mission. By following your instructions and example we will no doubt achieve success. It is guaranteed.

You have shown us that there is no greater happiness than seeing others take their first steps in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, because this brings the greatest happiness to the Lord. You exemplify the mood of a true Vaiṣṇava, of *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī,* unlimited compassion for others who are suffering. As Guru Dāsa once told you, “Sometimes when I see the suffering of others I become unhappy,” to which you replied, “Only sometimes, Guru Dāsa? Only sometimes?”

You have given us everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We pray that we can not only take wholeheartedly what you have given but that we can share our good fortune with others by continuing to spread your mission here in Scotland. Please give us the strength, the determination, and the inspiration to constantly rededicate our lives to practicing and preaching your teachings, side by side.

Please forgive our short-comings and continue to bestow your unlimited mercy upon us.

Your servants at Karuṇa Bhavan, Scotland.

## Kharghar

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We beg to offer our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, ISKCON Kharghar is one of the youngest temples in your ever-expanding worldwide movement, and we are very happy to make this offering at your lotus feet for the first time. We hope that you will be pleased to hear the report of our progress, because everything that we do is a direct result of your blessings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, although you did not wish to open many big temples, still they manifested in many parts of the world to broadcast the timeless message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your temples in Mumbai, Vṛndāvana, and Māyāpur, along with many other temples all over the world, attract so many different kinds of people and uplift the fallen souls of this age. Now, following in your footsteps and depending on your mercy, we are trying to establish the Glory of Maharashtra Project and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madanmohanjī Mandir here in Kharghar, Navi Mumbai. In a lecture you mentioned that there should be twelve temples in the greater Mumbai area, and we are doing our part to fulfill your desire.

We have a beautiful 8.5-acre plot in a prime location of Navi Mumbai, flanked by a majestic mountain range and verdant golf course on one side and the newly developed Central Park on the other. Surrounding the campus are many high-rise luxury apartments springing up in this quickly growing city. We plan to develop an entire spiritual center, with a beautiful temple for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madanmohanjī, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī *Śrī* *Sītā*-Rāma-Lakṣmaṇa-Hanumān. We also hope to establish a *prasādam* restaurant; a *vānaprastha*-*āśrama;* venues for musical and dramatic performances and light-and-sound shows; a spiritual art gallery; and a multidimensional showcase of Vaiṣṇava saints, *caitanya*-*līlā,* *kṛṣṇa*-*līlā,* and your own transcendental *līlā*. When completed, this mega project will serve as a spiritual oasis to soothe the parched, desertlike hearts and minds of the materialistic people of this city.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the task before us is tremendous, and we do not find the patience, perseverance, or ability within ourselves even to attempt it. As Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura has sung in one of his prayers to the spiritual master: “When I examine myself, I find nothing of value. Your mercy is therefore essential to me.” We are insignificant and undeserving of your compassion, yet your mercy is everything for us. We have taken this onerous challenge upon our heads because we believe in you and your divine mission. We bow down before your lotus feet and pray that, by your causeless mercy, we may become useful to you. Our only hope and fervent prayer is that we can become humble instruments of your compassion, dancing puppets in your pure hands.

This April (2011) we commemorated the second anniversary of the installation of the *utsava* Deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madanmohanjī and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and the establishment of a makeshift temple on our campus. Over the two-day festival, more than five thousand people from all over Navi Mumbai and other areas came to take *darśana* of the Lord, honor *prasādam,* and listen to *kṛṣṇa*-*kathā*. This achievement is certainly a manifestation of your immeasurable blessings for our own spiritual advancement and future progress.

We feel your presence with us every day—though your recorded instructions, through your books, and through your disciples and granddisciples. Your mercy sustains us and allows us to grow. Now we have thirty residential devotees and a growing congregation. Recently more than fifty devotees took *harināma* initiation from your dear disciple and the Governing Body Commissioner for this *yātrā*, His Holiness Gopāl Krishna Goswami—proving that your mercy is expanding as we try to become more sincere. Between three- and five-hundred devotees regularly attend the Sunday Feast program, the “love feast” you so ingeniously established. We realize that if we can simply follow your instructions with enthusiasm and humility, we can quickly achieve the ultimate success. We have several preaching programs, including special classes for youth, new *bhaktas*, and congregation members, to teach the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, because you taught us that “Preaching is the essence.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we collectively pray that we can work harder and more sincerely as humble ambassadors of your movement. We hope that we can cooperate as we strive to simply follow your teachings without any deviation. Without your mercy, we cannot achieve anything, as confirmed by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura:

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*

**“By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything we need, but we are sometimes stubborn and foolish and riddled with doubts. We hope that, as a loving father or grandfather forgives the offenses of his children or grandchildren, you will overlook our shortcomings and mistakes as we find the courage to become your true followers. Again and again we take refuge in the dust of your lotus feet, our only and everlasting shelter.

Your unworthy servants at ISKCON Kharghar, Navi Mumbai, Maharashtra, India.

## Klungkung

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*pañca-tattvātmakaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ bhakta-rūpa-svarūpakam*  
*bhaktāvatāraṁ bhaktākhyaṁ namāmi bhakta-śaktikam*

*jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*hare kṛṣṇa, hare kṛṣṇa, kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa, hare hare*  
*hare rāma, hare rāma, rāma rāma, hare hare*

O Paramaguru Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our obeisances at your merciful lotus feet. By your mercy we conditioned souls in this material world are receiving knowledge of self-realization, like light dissipating the darkness of ignorance.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we conditioned souls who are attached to material enjoyment have no qualifications at all for praising your unlimited glories, but by our devotional love we feel impelled to express a few words to glorify you on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, your mercy is flowing in Indonesia, especially in the island of Bali. Due to your books, your recorded teachings, and the guidance of your disciples, thousands of people here have become Kṛṣṇa conscious and are engaging in devotional service to Śrī *Gaurāṅga* and Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Your disciples regularly come to Bali and with full enthusiasm give us their blessings and guidance. As a result, in Bali there are now eight ISKCON temples and thousands of devotees. This enables us to maintain many programs, such as Śrī Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā, *harināma,* book distribution, Food for Life, Bali-Saṅkīrtan, preaching through Balinese cultural shows, and full observance of Vaiṣṇava festival days in each temple. We devotees in Bali feel transcendental bliss because we can be part of the *saṅkīrtana* movement, working to spread the holy name to every town and village in Bali and Indonesia. We devotees in Klungkung, a small town in the eastern part of Bali, due to your and your disciples’ mercy, and due to the enthusiasm and cooperation among the devotees, have been able to establish a temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjavihārī. The presiding Deities are Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjavihārī. And every day we glorify you by doing your *guru*-*pūjā*. *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!
