O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O Jagadguru, you have indeed been sent by Lord Kṛṣṇa to this world to take up the mission of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu: to spread the holy name to every town and village around the world and to save the fallen souls in this material world, such as us. You have proven your dedication and devotion to your guru, Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī* Mahārāja, by following his order to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western world. You have succeeded in introducing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to Western culture, which is full of passion (*rajas*) and ignorance (*tamas*). Millions of copies of your books have been distributed throughout the world, in many different languages; ISKCON has established many temples around the world; and most important, you have produced disciples who are dedicated to spreading your teachings through books, classes, and following the example you showed.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O Patita-pāvana, O destroyer of the sins of the fallen souls, you have given us the matchless gift of devotional love to Lord Kṛṣṇa (*kṛṣṇa-prema-bhakti*). By your mercy anyone can attain the perfection of spirituality as divine knowledge rises in his heart, giving *prema-bhakti* and destroying the ignorance. You are the *paramaguru,* an ocean of mercy, a friend of the fallen souls, and you are the guru for everyone and the life of all. O *paramaguru,* please give us your mercy and the shelter of your lotus feet. May your glories be sung throughout the three worlds.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day we pray that our aspiration to have a Vaiṣṇava village here in Bali will be fulfilled. We are very hopeful, since it is clear from your lectures that you also wanted your followers to establish exemplary Vaiṣṇava villages, in which devotees would live according to the principles of *varṇāśrama-dharma*. Please give your mercy to your disciples, especially the GBC and co-GBC of Indonesia, His Holiness Kavicandra Swami and His Holiness Rāmāi Swami, so that they will always enthusiastically visit Bali and guide us. By your mercy many ISKCON gurus have visited Bali to encourage us and guide us, and also to accept disciples. Some of them are His Holiness Gour Govinda Swami, His Holiness Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami, and His Holiness Bhaktirāghava Swami. In addition, many other devotees from abroad have mercifully given us their association, to encourage us to continue your mission and follow your teachings.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all your gifts and for inspiring so many of your senior disciples to show so much mercy to us here in Bali. May the whole world be at peace and enjoy the highest spiritual happiness by chanting the *mahā-mantra*:

hare kṛṣṇa, hare kṛṣṇa, kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa, hare hare  
hare rāma, hare rāma, rāma rāma, hare hare

Śrīla Prabhupāda kī jaya!

ISKCON guru-paramparā kī jaya!

Śrī Śrī Gauracandra-Nityānanda-rāma kī jaya!

Śrī Śrī Jagannātha-Baladeva-Subhadrā-devī kī jaya!

Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjavihārī kī jaya!

Your servants in Klungkung, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Kusumāpīḍa Dāsa and Ādipuruṣa Dāsa)

## Las Vegas

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances into the dust of your lotus feet.

This day of your Vyāsa-pūjā is the most auspicious day for all living entities on this earth planet! Just as the rising sun brings the auspiciousness of day by dissipating the darkness of night, similarly your appearance dissipates the darkness of Kali-yuga’s misguided, godless civilization and spreads the illumination of knowledge of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

For your followers, your Vyāsa-pūjā day is also a unique and wonderful opportunity to meditate on the glories of your lotus feet. Your lotus feet are the real shelter in this material world for thousands and thousands of conditioned souls suffering the reactions of their past misdeeds. Being unlimitedly compassionate, you have educated and rescued countless conditioned souls from the clutches of the illusory energy and helped them achieve liberation from the entanglement of material bondage.

Therefore the whole world is deeply indebted to Your Divine Grace for your hard work of bringing to human society the spiritual knowledge of God, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You not only imparted the purest spiritual knowledge of the Absolute Truth but also practically demonstrated how to implement this essential wisdom in day-to-day life.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, when I listen to your lectures, I am completely enchanted by the power of your conviction. I feel a strong need to surrender to Kṛṣṇa, a need so obvious and natural that I feel I have to do this immediately. The purifying and inspiring power of your words encourages me to become fully Kṛṣṇa conscious and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in total ecstasy. Your potent, inspiring words expressing your unlimited intimate knowledge of Kṛṣṇa have the power to motivate anyone to take up the process of *bhakti* and follow your instructions without the slightest doubt. This is the incredible potency of your unblemished purity and unconditional, deep love for Kṛṣṇa.

It is inconceivable how during your presence on this planet you had such immense power and vigor to propagate Kṛṣṇa consciousness yet remained truly humble, extremely kind, a perfect gentleman. For those who were not fortunate enough to meet you personally at that time and thus receive the mercy of your personal association, you have left volumes of your informative books. As you once said, “If you want to know me, read my books.” You even stated that your books are better than you because through your books you are making yourself available to future generations, for the next ten thousand years. Your books are as good as Kṛṣṇa. Anyone who even touches one of your books receives immense benefit.

So, despite the fact that I received your unlimited mercy three years after your departure from this planet, by reading your books I can relish every day the spiritual intimacy of your divine, enlightening association. Although I unfortunately suffered the loss of my *dīkṣā*-*guru* due to the attacks of *māyā,* I survived and was helped by the mercy of your divine ISKCON movement, which is full of devotees dedicated to your lotus feet. This year I am going to accept re-initiation from another disciple of yours, His Holiness Devāmrita Swami. He inspired me, guided me, and trained me in Kṛṣṇa consciousness from the very beginning of my joining your ISKCON movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the life of anyone who takes shelter at your lotus feet and follows the practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness becomes auspicious, regardless of any previous material circumstances. Thus only your unlimited causeless mercy has enabled your temple in Las Vegas to flourish within the last year. Not only were we able to move to a new, more convenient location, but we were also able to increase our temple programs for the benefit of suffering souls who are sincerely seeking genuine relief. Now not only do we have a Sunday Love Feast but also weekly *Bhagavad-gītā* classes and a daily morning program, with participation of devotees from outside. Since your last Vyāsa-pūjā, we held three major festivals and many smaller programs, with a total participation of hundreds of guests.

However, this capital of *Māyā*’s kingdom, Las Vegas, needs more of your mercy than any other place. Therefore, to make your mercy more accessible we plan to expand our preaching activities to a local university and share this supreme knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness with young people searching for the meaning of life.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the true strength behind our endeavors and the inspiration behind our preaching ideas. You are the intelligence behind our expansion plans and the love behind our compassionate behavior. You are the true lover of God. You are the true touchstone, since anyone who comes in contact with you becomes transformed from a sinner to a saint.

Please help us keep your lotus feet in our hearts so we will become immune to Māyās allurements and always take shelter of the holy names of the Lord.

Please help us always accept your unlimited mercy so we will become Kṛṣṇa conscious and empowered to help others become Kṛṣṇa conscious as well. Please bless us with the intelligence to always choose Kṛṣṇa and be fully engaged in loving devotional service to your lotus feet, according to your directives.

The humble and very grateful servants of your servants in Las Vegas, Nevada, USA.  
(written by Surapāla Dāsa)

## Ljubljana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I would like to take this opportunity to thank you, because all the spiritual knowledge I have received has come form your books, your lectures, and your followers. My last twenty years are based on your courage and determination to strictly follow the instructions of your spiritual master. Several times you declined to take credit for your preaching success, saying that the only things that enabled you to succeed were your strong faith in the holy name and in the instructions of your spiritual master. You fulfilled your goal in twelve years while you . . .

*Traveled around the world*  
*And cut with the śāstra*’s *sword*  
*The ignorance of Western world*  
*That is to sense gratification sold.*  
*At New Yoga Pīṭha*  
*On* vyāsāsan *you sit,*  
*And Padayātrā you lead*  
*At Pañca-tattva’s feet.*  
*By your divine grace*  
*We are able to face*  
*The difficult race*  
*That Māyā dictates.*  
*Chanting and singing*  
*The Lord’s holy name,*  
*We are spreading your fame*  
*Without any shame.*

Devotees here are extensively using multimedia to spread the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Representatives from the local TV station often visit the temple and broadcast some festival, or they prepare some spiritual show and ask us to comment. Recently they had a show called “Why Do Bad Things Happen to Good People?” We explained that it is due to the law of karma, that one has to suffer or enjoy the fruits of one’s work. But if one works for the pleasure of the Lord, we explained, then one is free from karmic reactions, good or bad.

Temple devotees are undergoing many book distribution marathons to raise *lakṣmī* so we can renovate the temple.

Every day lots of students come to Govinda’s restaurant to take *prasādam*.

We devotees in Slovenia beg your blessings so that we can be useful tools in spreading the mission of Lord Caitanya.

Your servants at New Yoga Pīṭha, Ljubljana, Slovenia.  
(written by Indulekhā Devī Dāsī)

## London (Kings Cross)

A million obeisances unto you,

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

To pray to remember  
All the mercy that I’ve had.  
To say that I’m a fool  
would be an understatement;  
my lack of taking the mercy  
Is my life’s one true embarrassment.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you created a society  
That could purify the devotee community.  
I must try harder to get the mercy,  
Before I miss this opportunity.  
I’m fully aware of the goal,  
I’m fully aware of your mercy,  
I’m fully aware for my faults,  
And aware of this controversy.  
I pray for the glorious day  
When I seriously bow to your feet  
And take your instructions seriously;  
Then my life will be complete.

Your servants in Kings Cross, London, UK.  
(written by Paraśurama Dāsa)

## London (Soho)

To our most dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories unto you!

All glories unto Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara!

Thank you for your causeless mercy and your compassionate love for the most undeserving and fallen. It is only through your words and the sincere devotees who follow you that we can learn about *kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* the ultimate happiness. All glories to you, for you are the bridge that carries us over the dangerous river of this material world.

Your disciples must take shelter of your lotus feet to achieve Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. And your mercy is nondifferent from the Lord’s mercy. Please be merciful to us.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, only because of you we have gotten Kṛṣṇa! Please send us back home, back to Kṛṣṇa—we implore you!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I truly believe your books save souls, and feeling indebted to you, I hope to serve you always by distributing these books, as you have truly saved my soul and shown me the light and truth in Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything, simply everything: the beautiful, breathtaking Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara and the jewels of Śrī Navadvīpa-dhāma, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, personalities who would otherwise have remained hidden from us; access to the most wonderful transcendental literature; a process to attain pure devotional service; and so much more.

O Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, although we will forever remain indebted to this very dear devotee of Yours for the causeless mercy he has shown us, please shower us with opportunities to serve His Divine Grace, and please empower us to always remember and never forget what Śrīla Prabhupāda has given us— everything.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your grace, many lost souls are returning home, back to Godhead. By your mercy we are learning how to serve Kṛṣṇa and break the shackles of ignorance.

You write in *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is,* “If we have a strong sense of love for the Supreme Lord, then we can discharge our duty and at the same time remember Him.” In our daily lives may we follow and share this principle, as you did.

Through your mercy we fallen souls have been given an opportunity to engage in devotional service. You said that book distribution “is our most important activity.” To please you, I pray that we continue to give Kṛṣṇa to all the fallen souls.

Your most grateful servants in Soho, London, UK.

## London (south)

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious day.

It is amazing how your mercy works. In so many different ways you engage all kinds of living entities in serving the Lord with devotion. It is your purity that enables you to do this. The following verse in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.1.15) wonderfully explains your qualities:

*yat-pāda-saṁśrayāḥ sūta munayaḥ praśamāyanāḥ*  
*sadyaḥ punanty upaspṛṣṭāḥ svardhuny-āpo 'nusevayā*

**“O Sūta, those great sages who have completely taken shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord can at once sanctify those who come in touch with them, whereas the waters of the Ganges can sanctify only after prolonged use.”**

Your disciples are so fixed up in carrying on with your mission of spreading the glories of the holy names that it feels as if Your Divine Grace were here, guiding all the *jīvātmās* back to Godhead. Recently we were blessed to have a twelve-hour *kīrtana* at the temple. It was hugely successful in increasing our devotional life, and we have only you to thank for making this possible. *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.13.10 explains the potency of pure devotees like Your Divine Grace:

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā*

**“My Lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.”**

Your legacy of opening up new temples and improving on existing ones is being carried out by the humble souls you have inspired. Soon we will see a beautiful temple open up in Mombasa, Kenya, a holiday destination for many people. For many devotees around the world this will be a spiritual haven, for a vist there will give them a break from the everyday hectic life of a devotee while allowing them to maintain or improve their *sādhana*. Temples such as these are nondifferent from the holy *dhāmas* in Bhārata-varṣa, and they enable your mission to flourish in every nook and cranny of the world. In this way more and more souls are being attracted to join Lord Caitanya’s movement. As stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.29.16):

*mad-dhiṣṇya-darśana-sparśa- pūjā-stuty-abhivandanaiḥ*  
*bhūteṣu mad-bhāvanayā sattvenāsaṅgamena ca*

**“The devotee should regularly see My statue in the temple, touch my feet, and offer worshipable paraphernalia and prayer. He should see in the spirit of renunciation, from the made of goodness, and see every living entity spiritual.”**

We cannot thank Lord Kṛṣṇa enough for sending you to this material world. It is only due to Your Divine Grace that we are able to get a glimpse of Goloka Vṛndāvana on this earth, and not just in places such as Māyāpur *dhāma,* Vṛndāvana, etc., but also in the hundreds of ISKCON temples around the world. You made sure that your disciples and granddisciples had every facility for improving their *sādhana* and staying fixed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Please keep on showering your mercy on all of us, helping us advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that one day we can all go back to Goloka Vṛndāvana and serve Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa to our heart’s content.

Your foolish disciples in South London, UK.

## Malaga

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, the *jagat-guru* and savior of the whole of humanity in this Age of Kali!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, no words will be sufficient to glorify you. Writing this offering is like offering Ganges water to the river Ganges.

We were very touched and felt ourselves honored to be asked to write this offering on behalf of our temple in Churriana, near Malaga, Spain. Over the past eighteen years, since coming in contact with the devotees, we have seen how you have touched so many people’s lives in different ways, even after leaving this planet. As you said, a Vaiṣṇava never dies but lives forever through his instructions.

In 1994, when the temple was about to be closed due to a lack of funds and very few visitors, suddenly, by your divine arrangement, you sent my wife Hema-gaurī Dāsī with a donation of $300 dollars. She explained to the president, Dharmarāja Prabhu, that she had collected the money from a children’s Diwalī show and that she wanted to give it as a donation. Hearing this, Dharmarāja asked her to arrange for some children to dance on Janmāṣṭamī. In this way she introduced her Indian friends and their families to the movement. With her involvement, our own family also met the devotees, and in due course we were all introduced to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

My father Puruṣottama Kṛṣṇa Dāsa was originally just an ordinary Hindu believer who worshiped Guru Nanak, but at the advanced age of sixty-five his whole life was transformed when he came in contact with your competent disciples Bhakti Chāru Swami and Girirāj Swami. Soon after my mother Bhagavatī Dāsī left her body five years ago, he settled in the holy land of Vṛndāvana, in a place just opposite the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandir. He strictly attended all the temple programs, chanted sixty-four rounds a day, and served the Vaiṣṇavas and the cows, which service became his only goal in life. Such is the potency of great Vaiṣṇavas like yourself, who can make the blind see and the lame walk over mountains. The day before my father left his body, he told us how Your Divine Grace had appeared to him in a dream very vividly and how you personally came to take him and my mother with you to Kṛṣṇa’s abode. This is your most merciful nature.

You also came in a dream to a German lady who had no idea who the Hare Kṛṣṇas were. One summer, at our Ratha-yātrā festival in Malaga, she saw your *mūrti* and was reminded of the person she had seen in her dream. She followed the devotees and finally ended up at the temple. Today she is known as Karuṇā-śakti Dāsī, an initiated disciple and a staunch member of your ISKCON movement.

Another lovely French couple, Murāri Govinda Dāsa and Kṛṣṇa-līlā Dāsī, became devotees when we were distributing *prasādam* in Marbella during a local fair. Abandoning all their old material routines, they shifted to the temple premises and for almost three years now have given themselves entirely to the service of the temple and the devotees.

Another Indian lady, presently called Lāvaṇya-mayī Dāsī, who at first was not at all interested in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, got hold of your *Bhagavad-gītā* on CD, which she posted to her son in America. He became inquisitive to learn about Kṛṣṇa and thus got in touch with some local devotees. Soon the whole family—the two children and their respective spouses and parents—all became wonderful full-time devotees.

Even I, although coming from the business community and still being an active businessman, have received brahminical initiation, and I am actively preaching to the Indian community of Malaga and Gibraltar. It sounds unbelievable, but still it is fact, and all this has been made possible by your causeless mercy.

Around the year 2004–05 we had a setback in our temple due to the lack of proper leadership, which caused us enormous anxiety. We urgently tried to find dedicated devotees to take care of the daily functions, because otherwise the only alternative was to open the temple only on Sundays, just for the feast, when *gṛhastha* families living outside could participate. A request was sent all over Europe, but for months there was no response. Our anxieties multiplied with each passing day as we kept a close watch on our email inbox to see if there was any luck. Finally, with your magic working from behind the scenes, Vedavyāsa Prabhu and his wife Rasāmṛta Dāsī, along with their daughter *Gaurāṅga*-līlā Dāsī and her husband Caitanya-candra Dāsa, took charge of the temple and, with great ease and grace, brought it under control in a very short time. Today, thanks to them, the temple is running very smoothly, with a constant congregation of about sixty devotees and another fifty devotees who visit off and on.

During this year’s Gaura Pūrṇimā festival we installed, as an expansion of the existing set of small brass Gaura-Nitāi Deities, a set of beautiful big neem Gaura-Nitāi Deities. To accommodate Their Lordships on the altar, His Holiness Bhakti Chāru Swami donated a magnificent *śṛṅgāsana* hand-carved from teak wood, which was shipped to us from Ujjain under his careful supervision.

There are so many diversities in this movement, but, above all, everything is spiritual and devotional. People have become devotees through books, *prasādam,* and paraphernalia, and even by just coming to the temple on the pretext of buying some locally grown vegetables.

Thank you so much, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for establishing such a wonderful organization, which is so perfect and complete in every aspect. We pray for the protection of our small congregation, and we beg for your mercy and blessings to be able to help you in your mission to pull more and more souls into this great movement of congregational chanting of the sweet holy names of Lord Kṛṣṇa, as inaugurated by Lord *Gaurāṅga,* and thus relieve them of their miseries.

Your most fallen servants at the ISKCON temple in Malaga, Spain.  
(written by Vinod Vihārī Dāsa and Hema-gaurī Dāsī)

## Melbourne

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.**

*śrī-caitanya-mano-’bhīṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikam*

**When will Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī Prabhupāda, who has established within this material world the mission to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya, give me shelter under his lotus feet?**

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**I offer my respectful obeisances at the lotus feet of my spiritual master, by whose mercy a dumb man can become an eloquent speaker and a lame man can cross mountains.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

As Kali-yuga rolls on, imperceptibly crushing all good qualities before it, there is an eddy, a current, a rising tide that is bucking its trend. From whence did it come? From the efforts of one unique soul—you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are unique in your single-handed effort to fulfill the order of Guru and *Gaurāṅga*. You are a special ambassador of the spiritual world, carrying the message of Śrī Krsna and Śrī Caitanya to one and all without discriminating. You ventured to a place incongruous for holy men of pure goodness, the Lower East Side of Manhattan, to plant the seeds of *bhakti* in the hearts of men and women so far from Goloka Vṛndāvana, trapped in the depths of material ignorance.

From nowhere, it seems, you established the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Who could imagine? Certainly none of the followers of your then fledgling Society could fathom what was going on. Why, even fifty years on we have trouble understanding your vision. We imagine we are following your orders, only to discover years later that we only understood a minute fraction of what you taught and gave.

You knew exactly what you were doing.

“I am not a poor man. I am rich. There are temples and books; they are existing, they are there, but the time is separating us from them.”

As we participate in your Society and watch it grow, we can’t help but admire how your efforts are coming to fruition. There are problems, just as there were problems when you were present. Yet the momentum you started at 26 Second Avenue on the Lower East Side, which traveled west, east, north and south, now truly spans the globe. Obstacles appear, but just as a tsunami propelled by subterranean forces can’t be stopped, so similarly your ISKCON moves forward, imperceptibly, unstoppable, forceful, occasionally wavering, but wherever there are devotees dedicated to following your orders, it continues to grow. It started with your clear vision, the printing and distribution of books. Not ordinary books but spiritually potent books describing the original message of the eternal truth, books imbued with a vision of the workings of the material and spiritual worlds, the processes of liberation, and the pinnacle of human achievement, Krsna consciousness, the culmination of the science of self-realization.

Just as a mighty banyan tree grows from a small seed, so your International Society grew from one small center to many in a matter of a few short years. Your books also grew. After *Back to Godhead* magazine came *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā,* then *Teachings of Lord Caitanya,* Kṛṣṇa book, *The Nectar of Devotion,* more volumes of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and then *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. Enough learning for everyone to practice and perfect spiritual understanding, and a society of devotees in which to put it to the test.

By steady application of that simple process of following your instructions, everything grew. It is simply a matter of time before it grows further.

Just as you had a vision of what you wanted to achieve, you knew also that those who follow your instructions would traverse the same terrain covered by the previous saints and, despite obstacles, achieve success.

You are a special personality. A rare, unique, once-in-a-millennium person, the likes of whom will not be seen for many thousands of years to come.

In meditating on Your Divine Grace, we pray that we may become like you. Not that we can at all match your transcendental qualities. We live in hope that by your association we can catch the mercy you so clearly hold and magnanimously give away, that we can hold and follow your divine instructions, for our benefit and for the benefit of the world.

Please accept us as your servants. We pray to be worthy of your grace.

Your servants at ISKCON’s Mahāprabhu Mandir in Melbourne, Australia.  
(written by Aniruddha Dāsa)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Once, when responding to glorification that you had written so many wonderful books, you humbly replied, “Drops of water wear away the stone.” You were referring to your daily regimen of spending six hours daily writing books in the early morning. You would write only a few pages each day, but over a period of weeks, months, and years you wrote many large volumes of transcendental literature.

Practically, that one statement describes your mood in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You did not come to this country with a large bank balance, lots of followers, or any other kind of material support. You came only with your pure desire to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, under the order of your spiritual master. And just as water constantly and perpetually dripping on a stone wears it away, little by little you have been wearing away the stonelike coverings of our unwilling hearts by consistently delivering the message of Lord Caitanya.

You have set your ISKCON movement in motion. You stated that “This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will continue to increase for the next ten thousand years.” This movement will spread, not overnight by violent revolution, but one person at a time. And over time, millions of people will become Kṛṣṇa conscious. The most important thing is consistency. *The Nectar of Instruction* states that enthusiasm, confidence, and patience are all necessary for us to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. Enthusiasm without patience is in the mode of passion, and patience without enthusiasm is in the mode of ignorance. So enthusiasm and patience, together with confidence born of faith in Lord Kṛṣṇa, are the perfect combination to ensure that we become Kṛṣṇa conscious. It is also the secret to becoming a successful preacher. In the *Bhagavad-gītā (*9.22*)* Lord Kṛṣṇa states:

*ananyāś cintayanto māṁ ye janāḥ paryupāsate*  
*teṣāṁ nityābhiyuktānāṁ yoga-kṣemaṁ vahāmy aham*

**“But those who always worship Me with exclusive devotion, meditating on My transcendental form—to them I carry what they lack, and I preserve what they have.”**

This verse is very significant. For a pure devotee like your good self, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can easily see how Lord Kṛṣṇa carried what you appeared to lack materially due to old age, ill health, lack of qualified followers, and other considerations. He empowered you to do the impossible—to transplant Vedic culture to the land of the *yavanas* and *mlecchas*. You were empowered because, as a pure devotee, you unconditionally dedicated your mind, intelligence, body, and words to the service of the Lord, and He gave you all you needed to succeed.

Similarly, as your servants, we can also become empowered to do wonderful work on your behalf, to the degree that we can fix our consciousness on the service of the Lord. You said, “Actually, Kṛṣṇa does not care for how much we give to Him, but He sees how much we are keeping back for ourselves.” Lord Kṛṣṇa will carry what we lack, but we need to be sure that we are really lacking it. Lord Kṛṣṇa knows what each of us is capable of and if we are really giving Him all we can. Externally, what each of us can give varies, but the goal is to give the Lord everything we can. If we do that, then He will carry what we lack and make our Kṛṣṇa conscious endeavors a success.

This is actually quite encouraging and enlivening. We each determine how surrendered we will be, and based on that surrender to the Lord we can become empowered to succeed and serve your mission, as you served your spiritual master’s mission. This movement is not lost, nor will it ever fail. Any person in contact with this movement for the next ten thousand years can succeed if he or she accepts and applies to their own lives the knowledge you gave us. There are no material impediments that can prevent any of us from becoming pure devotees of the Lord if we desire that goal. We need to see daily how we can take advantage of our individual situations to help us become Kṛṣṇa conscious. Especially once we’ve committed ourselves by taking initiation, Lord Kṛṣṇa takes charge of our lives. Our lives may bring us material happiness or distress, but in any condition we can utilize our life’s situation for making spiritual advancement, if that is our goal.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as you taught and practiced the principle of “Drops of water wear away stone,” so we can attempt to follow your example. For us the drops of water are our daily chanting of sixteen rounds, following the four regulative principles, studying your books, helping our the local temple, and working in a cooperative way with the temple management and other devotees to serve your mission. If we just carry out these simple endeavors regularly, whether we feel like it or not, the result will be that each of us will become Kṛṣṇa conscious and we will become empowered to serve you and your mission wonderfully. Thank you for giving us this wonderful opportunity, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Miami, Florida, USA.

## Montreal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this glorious day of your appearance we take the time to reflect on our relationship with you. Despite the joyous characteristic of such a day, our position is very contradictory, since we remain stunned and gladdened (conscious) miserable beggars in front of their king. We are poor and indebted fellows assembled at your lotus feet. If there is one day throughout the year to be humble, this is it—today, on your Vyāsa-pūjā day. And the humbler we are, the happier we are! We don’t mind. Provided you are glorified to our heart’s content.

Usually, from a material point of view, a miserable person has no reason to be stunned and gladdened in front of the nation’s king, because his natural tendency is to blame others, criticizing them and cursing them because of negative whims of a lowborn destiny. This is especially true in Kali-yuga, when everyone is a lowborn *śūdra*. No amount of materialistic education can cover up this lowborn characteristic of men and women born in Kali-yuga.

But in Kali-yuga the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa sometimes sends a real leader for society. A real role-model king for such unfortunate souls. An empowered preacher whose followers, within no time, begin to become truly educated about the real values of life and who act accordingly. After a short while, such persons become effulgent, wear *tilaka,* and dress as Vaikuṇṭha men or women, and when they open their mouths there issues forth a flow of nectarean descriptions of the glories of the Supreme Lord and His sublime service offered by His innumerable servants in the best of conditions, in the spiritual world, or unconditionally and uninterruptedly in the material world. Gradually, but with greater enthusiasm as days go by, such persons begin to revive their divine qualities, which had remained dormant within for such a long time. Slowly, they recover from the nasty anesthesia of material existence en route to their normal, healthy state of life.

But who has made all of this possible? It is not the Santa of Western folklore but the real *santa,* or saint, of modern times—you, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*. Being a true *santa,* a pure devotee of the Lord, you are also *śānta,* perfectly peaceful, because, as we sing every day, you are *viṣṇu-pādāya,* i.e., you have taken complete shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. And because of that steady and perfect shelter, you are *abhaya,* one who has no fear of anything. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are truly *patita-pāvana,* a friend of poor souls like us.

We find all of those characteristics in you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and because of the education we have been incredibly fortunate enough to embrace in the form of your sublime teachings, we have reawakened a bit to our constitutional position. Nonetheless, we should not forget our previous fallen condition and should feel eternally indebted to you. We are like the characters that Victor Hugo would have suitably called “the miserables.” We admit our low birth, but nonetheless by your causeless mercy, by the power of the spiritual knowledge we have received from you, we have recovered enough sanity to feel enormously grateful to you, whom we accept as our worthiest leader, our *śikṣā* or *dīkṣā* guru, the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, a society of practicing devotees, of sincere, genuine, aspiring positive *bhakti-yogīs*.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, despite our being in the material atmosphere, you have indicated to us in so many encouraging ways that we should fully embrace the taste of eternity in this very lifetime within the *saṅkīrtana* movement by chanting the holy name in *kīrtana* and in *japa;* by cooking, offering, and partaking of *prasādam;* and by *bhajana, kīrtana,* *kṛṣṇa-kathā,* *sevā,* *sat-saṅga,* transcendental literature production and distribution, etc. You have given us education, you have given us engagement, you have given us proper, worthy, healthy invaluable association, and you are still sharpening our judgment to properly focus on the ultimate goal of life, the Supreme Person, the most attractive Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, thus putting an end to all our doubts about real worthy life. Therefore today we are feeling extremely grateful at your lotus feet, and we beg to ever be mindful of the shelter afforded by your tender lotus feet. Even though we may not be very well educated from an alpha or omega point of view, it does not matter as much as our attitude of devotion to follow in your footsteps, which means nothing less than to be a servant of your wide-open heart, ever flowing with compassion toward all the suffering conditioned souls.

Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we don’t mind being described as miserables. This is just a sign of proper understanding of our true fallen condition, whereby we are compelled to embrace your cause, the cause of spreading Kṛṣṇa awareness or Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which is the only real engagement of anyone who has understood your sincere and deepest desire: to save all the fallen conditioned souls for the sake of Kṛṣṇa by taking part in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

On this auspicious day we pray to remain sheltered at your lotus feet and to fully understand three fundamental principles: 1) *sevya,* the immediate object of our service, namely their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara; 2) *sevā,* how to serve their Lordships intelligently, obediently, and with purity, seriousness, and sincerity, avoiding offenses; 3) *sevaka,* the servitor with blessed understanding of what it means to be a servitor of their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara in the assembly of devotees.

We are not perfect, but for miserables like us, you are our perfect model to follow. By the mercy of both Your Divine Grace and Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara, we know beyond any doubt that in this life we have already tasted eternity and that we will never be satisfied with anything less than that.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us what nobody else could ever have given us, namely the higher taste of being fixed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. On this most memorable day of your appearance, we beg to reciprocate with you by rendering you fully surrendered, dedicated, unmotivated service in the blissful assembly of similarly-minded devotees. We remain your fortunate beggars of such precise mercy. The taste you have given us drives us that way. We believe we are becoming mad after Kṛṣṇa consciousness, that highest taste of which we cannot give up anymore. Thanks so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your causeless mercy. May we join you again for eternity.

Your ever-grateful servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara Mandir, Montreal, Canada.  
*(*written by Prābhava Vigraha Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet.

Today we remember you most through your superlative books and the divine instructions you have given us through them. In many of your purports, letters, and lectures, you have instructed us to chant a minimum of sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* daily. You have also given us the four regulative principles and lovingly assured us that if we can chant our rounds without offense and observe these regulations sincerely, Kṛṣṇa will take us back home, back to Godhead in this lifetime. At such difficult times in the Kali-yuga as these, through these simple instructions you have given all of us an opportunity to be elevated to the eternal devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have also mercifully taught us the sacred principle of being a humble servant of the devotees. In a *Bhagavad-gītā* purport (13.26) you write, “One should learn to become the servant of those who are in knowledge of the Supreme Lord. If one is fortunate enough to take shelter of a pure devotee, hear from him about self-realization, and follow in his footsteps, one will be gradually elevated to the position of a pure devotee.” Through the wonderful association of devotees (*sādhu-saṅga*) at your lovingly created ISKCON centers, you have given all of us unlimited opportunity to perfect our lives by hearing about Lord Kṛṣṇa. It’s in this *saṅga* that following the regulative principles and chanting our rounds become an easy and happy experience.

You personally followed these principles and established the highest standard of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your example proves that the Kṛṣṇa conscious lifestyle is fulfilling and most satisfying for any soul. You were the personification of true spiritual bliss; even in the midst of serious calamities like the heart attacks you faced or the envious people in the initial days in Juhu, you always remembered Kṛṣṇa and sung the Lord’s glories happily.

These wonderful, transcendental qualities of yours give us hope that if we can somehow remain connected to you and your wonderful devotees, we can survive the cyclonic attacks of *māyā*. We are spiritually weak and are struggling with *anarthas,* and we are also driven by our false ego, yet your causeless mercy can take even us unqualified souls back home, back to Godhead.

Please continue to shower your divine mercy on us, your spiritual grandchildren, and help us stick to the basic rules and follow the advanced devotees, your disciples. We want to please you by following these instructions of yours as our life and soul.

Your unworthy servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Temple, Chowpatty, Mumbai, India.  
(written by Śrī Nāthjī Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Juhu)

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

For the year 2010 ISKCON Juhu stood in the third position in the world in your transcendental book distribution program. Please bless us to increase our book distribution.

The construction of the new building of the Bhaktivedanta Swami Mission School is already completed. Please bestow your mercy so we may shift the present school at Hare Krishna Land to this new building as soon as possible.

By your mercy the life membership program is going well. We also re-started the Saturday evening program for life members. Please bless us to make them more and more Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Our student preaching program, known as the Centre for the Heritage of Indian Culture and Education (CHOICE) is developing nicely in Powai, Surat, Raipur, Jalgaon, Kopargaon, and Jaipur. Please bless the students to become wonderful servants of your beloved Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī.

In pursuit of your vision for systematic study of the Vedic scriptures, we are offering a Bhakti Śāstrī course. Last year fourteen devotees were awarded the Bhakti Śāstrī degree. A new batch of students has enrolled for the Bhakti Śāstrī course, and your blessing is required to make this program successful.

We are making good progress in the farm project at Talasari. Your blessing is essential to tap a water resource at the land.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for engaging us in the service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Juhu, Mumbai, India.

## Mumbai (Mira Road)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Rupa Goswami writes in his *Stava-mālā*:

*yad api samādhiṣu vidhir api paśyati na tava nakhāgra-marīcim*  
*idam icchāmi niśamya tavācyuta tad api kṛpādbhuta-vīcim*

**“Even** ***Brahmā*** **in full** ***samādhi*** **cannot catch a glimpse the effulgence emanating from the tips of Your toenails. Still, O Acyuta, because I have heard of the waves of Your astonishing mercy, I long to see that effulgence.**

*bhaktir udañcati yadyapi mādhava na tvayi mama tila-mātrī*  
*parameśvaratā tad api tavādhika-durghaṭa-ghaṭana-vidhātrī*

**“O Mādhava! Although I don’t possess even a sesame seed of** ***bhakti*** **for You, still, since Your inconceivable power makes the impossible possible, please fulfill the heart’s deepest desires for that** ***bhakti*****.”**

Similarly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, although it seems impossible for us to become pure devotees, you can make the impossible possible. So, we are cherishing a hope against hope that we can become pure devotees and serve you exclusively for your pleasure and the pleasure of the Supreme Lord Śrīla Prabhupāda, your purports bring life to the verses of the *śāstra*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all twenty-six qualities of pure devotees are found in you. We would like to highlight three of these qualities, namely “caring,” “clean,” and “compassionate.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an emblem of compassion, as shown by your overwhelming compassion for us. “Overwhelming compassion” means that the person feeling this compassion has so much love for the objects of his compassion (the poor conditioned souls) that his actions are controlled by that love. How concerned you were with the welfare of your disciples! You were never too busy to make sure your disciples were well cared for. Thus you taught us compassion by your personal example. Caring for devotees is indeed the most important component for the success of ISKCON’s preaching. Please bless us so that we will love all of Kṛṣṇa’s devotees. We will do everything within our means to set up whatever systems are necessary to ensure that all the devotees are taken care of now and in the future.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your cleanliness was so profound and far-reaching that you were able to purify the lives of thousands of men and women who previously had no proper concept of spiritual or personal cleanliness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you had firm determination to preach the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you had absolute conviction of the spiritual truth, and you had unflagging determination to push forth this ISKCON movement against all odds. Your strength gives all your followers strength. You made an immense sacrifice for us, taking responsibility for bringing us back to the spiritual world. Your books are the law books that guide our daily lives, and the association with you we experience by reading your books every day is the lifeline of our spiritual advancement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda you are Surrendered to Śrī Kṛṣṇa

- Respectful
- Intelligent
- Loving
- Awesome
- Peaceful
- Reliable
- Ambitious for Kṛṣṇa
- Bold
- Happy to see Kṛṣṇa consciousness spread
- Understanding
- Pure
- Appreciative for any service
- Determined
- Always thinking of Śrī Kṛṣṇa

You had your grand strategy. You saw that everyone was chasing America. If you could turn America toward Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you would turn the world. Others said it was impossible. You said, “*Impossible* is a word in a fool’s dictionary.” Because of your complete confidence in Kṛṣṇa’s ever-presence and His unlimited potency, you were fearless. Once, in New York, your disciples advised you to take a break for rest and recuperation. You responded by saying, “Don’t take away my privilege to struggle for Kṛṣṇa.” Your example is very important for all present and future devotees of this movement.

**Report of ISKCON MiraRoad**

(1) Temple construction: Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy the temple construction is progressing nicely. The new temple hall is ready, and we celebrated Śrī Gaura Pūrṇimā there.

(2) *Saṅkīrtana:* Encouraged by the blessings of His Holiness Gopāl Krishna Mahārāja, we were able to distribute 35,000 *Bhagavad-gītās* during the 2010 marathon.

(3) Youth preaching: More than one hundred boys come every month for the Upāsanā Festival, which is exclusively conducted for boys aged 15 to 25. Our *brahmacārīs* are preaching in and around Mira Road, apart from having regular programs for young people in the temple.

(4) Children preaching: We started a new Saturday school for children in Kandivali, and more than 150 regularly attend. By your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our Youth Forum for Girls and NICE institute for children are also running smoothly.

(5) Counselor system: The counseling system is gradually developing, with fifteen counselors and about five hundred counselees registered.

(6) Ratha-yātrā: We had Ratha-yātrās at Mira Road, Thane, Bhayender, and Kandivali.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would like to do more—more for you, more for Lord Kṛṣṇa. There is so much more to do: more preaching, more *harināma,* more *saṅkīrtana,* more *prasādam* distribution. Please help us by inspiring us and endowing us with spiritual strength. Please help us really work together. We depend on your mercy every day, and every moment.

No matter how busy we may be, our activities should always revolve around you, and our attachment to your lotus feet should increase with the passing of time, not decrease.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us that we can become effective instruments in this wonderful mission you have established, and please allow us to be engaged in your service life after life. Bless us so that we can serve your lotus feet steadily, just as our Guru Mahārāja is serving your holy instructions.

You famously said that the way we can show our gratitude for all you have done for us is to cooperate in pushing on this movement. So we are trying to cooperate, and we hope all the devotees will cooperate for Your Divine Grace’s pleasure.

On this auspicious day we pray to Your Divine Grace to please bless us all with the intelligence, determination, tolerance, flexibility, and Kṛṣṇa consciousness to overcome our differences and cooperate fully, as you desired, so that ISKCON may flourish and thus provide more and more devotees and conditioned souls with the chance to take shelter in your great society.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Mira Road, Mumbai, India.

## Nagara-Saṅkīrtana Mandir

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda  
Your teachings.  
Are like the cintāmaṇi stones in Vaikuṇṭha.  
Your words . . .  
Are as powerful as the thunderbolt that attacked Govardhana Hill.  
Your mercy . . .  
Is as strong as the shower of devotional love.  
Your smile . . .  
Is like the blossoming flowers in the forests of Vṛndāvana.  
Your books . . .  
Are like a sword cutting the handcuffs of Māyā-devī.  
Your appearance . . .  
Is the destroyer of material attachment.  
Your presence . . .  
Is an unlimited shower of mercy.  
Your coming to the West . . .  
Is enthusiasm.  
Your existence . . .  
Is hope.  
And . . .  
Your Vyāsa-pūjā . . .  
Is your love that penetrates all boundaries.  
O jewel of the Vedas . . .  
O the jewel of bhakti,  
Your life is for the eternal,  
Your sincerity changes the world,  
Your faithfulness attracts the heart,  
And . . .  
Your works  
Are a source of inspiration.

Your servants at Nagara-Saṅkīrtana Mandir, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Maheśa Paṇḍita Dāsa)

## Nagpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to you on your Vyāsa-pūjā day. All glories to Śrī Guru and Śrī Gaurāṅga.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, many good things have transpired in ISKCON Nagpur during the last year. We had the best book marathon ever in our history, distribute more than eight thousand *Bhagavad-gītās* during the month of December. Our distribution teams went out every day.

A stronger and more united management team has been established in Nagpur, so that more care and spiritual guidance can be given to the temple devotees, as well as the congregation members.

Just a month ago, due to the ceaseless *tapasya* and never-ending enthusiasm of our beloved Guru Mahārāja, His Holiness Lokanāth Swami, and your eternal and causeless mercy upon all of us, we were offered a completely new temple building, with all facilities, right in the center of Nagpur. We are now in the process of taking over this wonderful property, and we have high hopes this move will further advance the preaching mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We have also acquired some land in another very promising location, right next to Nagpur’s international airport. We hope to build a beautiful temple there for the pleasure of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, making it a distinct landmark for the city of Nagpur.

We clearly see Kṛṣṇa’s hand at work. When we are united, everything becomes auspicious and wonderful.

From the story of the Pracetas in the Fourth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* we can understand that Śrī Kṛṣṇa is bestowing the same mercy upon us as He bestowed on them when they stayed unified during their ten thousand years of *tapasya* underwater. You famously said, “You will show your love for me by the way you cooperate once I am gone,” and we are trying, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

The only way we will be able to achieve our goals in Nagpur is if you shower your mercy on us. First we need your causeless mercy and the mercy of our entire *guru*-*paramparā,* and only then can we expect the mercy of Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa. We therefore most humbly and sincerely pray at your lotus feet that you will empower us to reach out to one another and to all the souls in Nagpur. Please give us a little taste for the holy name, and please forgive all our shortcomings and offenses at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī* *jaya*! You are the savior of the most fallen.  
Your humble servants at ISKCON Nagpur, India.

## New Badarikāśrama

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your inconceivable service to *Śrī* Kṛṣṇa.

The *śāstra* informs us that hearing about your glory is so unlimited that it is far beyond the stage of *brahmānanda,* so we ordinary fallen souls can get only a glimpse of understanding about the mercy of the dust from your feet from authorized scriptures and great self-realized personalities. Through the eyes of the authorized scriptures you have so magnanimously presented to us, we can, only by your causeless mercy, acquire some comprehension of your unimaginable glory.

Many of us have had the good fortune to associate with you personally through your books, recorded lectures, and videos. It is said that contact with a pure devotee such as you for even a fraction of a moment, even without formally receiving any teachings, will remove any attraction one might have to the results of pious activity or the culture of so-called knowledge. So now what interest can we have in the rewards of material life, except for engaging all such rewards in the propagation your transcendental cause, this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. We can follow in the footsteps of Lord Śiva and beg the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa to bless us with your association, for you are always merciful upon fallen conditioned souls. We do not have your physical company at this time, and therefore our real benediction is to take shelter of your books and the processes you have prescribed for reviving our dormant love for Kṛṣṇa.

There are many processes for understanding the Absolute Truth, but for us the only way the Absolute Truth will be revealed is if we receive your mercy. It is you who have kindly given us the invitation to enter into the eternal festival of spiritual life.

What good fortune to have achieved a human birth on this earth during this time of the inauguration of your ISKCON movement! There is no use striving for material gain to enjoy profuse material comforts. Such a life bars one from taking shelter under the shade of your lotus feet.

Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, informs us in the Eleventh Canto about you, stating how dullness, fear, and ignorance are destroyed by engaging in the service of a pure devotee. The Lord tells us the pure devotee is peacefully fixed in absolute knowledge and is the ultimate shelter for souls like us, who are repeatedly rising and falling within the fearful ocean of material life. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are indeed just like a strong boat who have come to rescue us, who were on the point of drowning in the sea of perplexities.

Just as food is the life of all creatures, just as Kṛṣṇa is the ultimate shelter of the distressed, and just as religion is the wealth of those who are passing away from this world, you, Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee, are the only refuge for persons like us, who are fearful of falling into a miserable condition of life.

Whereas the sun allows only external sight, and that only when risen in the sky, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can bestow divine vision, like that Kṛṣṇa gave Arjuna to behold His universal form. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, intimate servant of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, you are our real worshipable deity and real family. You are all we are made of, and ultimately you are nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa.

We may not have your personal association (*vapu*), but we can relish your companionship in a superior way by scrutinizingly studying your books and hearing from your bona fide representatives about your instructions (*vāṇī*). In this way, free from mental concoction, free from the mind’s agitation due to accepting and rejecting, and free from intellectual wranglings, we can achieve the perfection of love for Kṛṣṇa

All of us beg for your mercy to make progress on the path back home, back to Godhead. We wish to show this advancement by cooperating with authorities and other devotees while rendering our various services.

Thank you so very much.

Your eternal debtors at New Badarikāśrama, Denver, Colorado, USA.

## New Biharvan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you, and all glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkebihārī, the presiding Deities of New Biharvan, Boise.

Every day, every year in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is special. This year, 2011, saw the 525th anniversary of Lord Caitanya’s appearance; today is the 115th anniversary of your most divine appearance; in July we will celebrate the 45th anniversary of the incorporation of ISKCON; and, in a very small measure, this year will see the 25th anniversary of the founding of our temple in Boise.

As all the activities on this planet are happening because of the presence of the sun, so we celebrate all these anniversaries because of you.

The body of ISKCON runs because you are the heart of ISKCON. Here in Boise we would have not survived one year, what to speak of twenty-five years, had it not been for your divine, mystical presence here.

We still remember the first true Sunday Feast we held in Boise, in November of 1986. We had observed devotees in Los Angeles and Chicago do it. Now it was our turn. Sitting on a cushioned chair, you watched us struggle in a small kitchen.

The phone rang. Janakīnātha Prabhu was on the other end. “I am in Boise. How do I get to the temple?” He had shepherded us into Kṛṣṇa consciousness through your books. We had lost touch with him. How did he know we were here? Janakīnātha Prabhu came and showed us how to do a Sunday feast. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

A year later, a disciple of Vīrabāhu Prabhu named Prabhupādasevā Dāsa showed up at our door. We had met him very briefly in Los Angeles. In the early days of our temple, every time a devotee visited us in Boise, we knew you had sent him or her. Something needed to be done for our survival. Sure enough, Prabhupādasevā Prabhu was an accountant by trade and thus knew about incorporating companies. He told us how we should incorporate. He stayed with us for a week, making sure all the paperwork was submitted and approved. As his name implied, he had come to do *sevā* for you—to make sure that your temple was on sound legal ground. His legal work has guided us through these twenty-five years. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We made our first devotees—Brijdevī *Mātājī* and Sādhumārga Prabhu—when she miraculously got her vision back and he ate thirty *gulabjāmons,* your “ISKCON bullets,” in one sitting. You sent us Mother Yaśodāmāyī with our first Jagannātha Deities and the skill to do our plays at Ratha-yātrā, Mother Revatī Devī Dāsī with the big Jagannātha Deities and the ability to sew the dresses and huge curtain for the altar. Mother Īśvarī painted Them; Kāmagāyatrī *Mātājī* got us our own *ratha*. You sent Āśā, Śaśijījī, Ṣaḍbhuja Dāsa, and Ārati Devī Dāsī as our order-suppliers in India, our two preaching faces, and four sets of helping hands, and you sent Rādhikāramaṇa, Gopālhari, and Kamalānanda Prabhu to keep your temple brand new. How can we forget the *sevās* rendered by Tulasī Kṛṣṇa, Mādhurya Rādhā*,* Mālatī Mātājī, and Shyāmjī? The temple became a community with Amṛta *Mātājī*, Śrīhari, Śrīnivās, Upendra, Balabhadra, Candra Prabhu, and our *bhaktas* and *bhaktins—*Anurag, Garnett, Jayant, Nalini Steve, and Tim—who have settled with families near the temple and serve the Deities. These are just a few of the many devotees you sent to help us keep the temple running. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all the devotees.

Above all, you sent four of your best assistants to hold our hands and guide us—Badrinārāyaṇ Prabhu, Gaṇapati Swami, Gopāl Krishna Goswami, and Hanumatpresaka Swami. Year after year, for twenty-five years, they have guided us and reminded us of your presence and your divine mission, and prodded us to carry on. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Our biggest fear in this remote Idaho desert was how we would handle all the trials and tribulations that every temple goes through. In hindsight, our fears were unfounded. We realize you were with us all the time.

*One n*ight I had a dream . . .

*I dreamed I was walking in the hot desert sands of Idaho*.  
*I was walking along the desert with you*.  
*Across the sky flashed scenes from the temple.*  
*For each scene I noticed two* sets of footprints in the sand:  
*One belonged to me, and the other to you.*  
*Twenty-*five years later,  
*I looked back at the footprints in the sand.*  
*I noticed* that many times along the path  
*There was only one set of footprints.*  
*I also noticed that it happened at the very lowest and saddest times in* the life of the temple.  
*This really bothered me, and I questioned* Baṅkebihārī about it.  
*“*Lord, You said that once I decided to follow You,  
*You would walk with me all the way;*  
*But I have noticed that during the mo*st troublesome times  
*There is only one set of footprints.*  
*I don*’t understand why in times when I  
*needed You the most, You should leave me.*”  
*Ba*ṅkebihārī replied, “My innocent child,  
*I love you, and I would never,*  
*never leave you during your times of*  
*trial and suffering.*  
*When you saw only one set of footprints,*  
*It was then that My Prabhupāda was carrying you.*”

(adapted from “Footprints in the Sand,” by Mary Stevenson)

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for carrying all of us in your lap, in Boise.

Stuck in the hot material sands of Idaho, we remain  
Your servants in Boise, Idaho, USA.

## New Dvārākā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

A few years ago a disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda passed away, and just before he left his body his Christian mother told him, “Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to *Prabhupāda*!” That is the inspiration for the following poem.

***“*****Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”**

We were searching for the highest of highs,  
Not knowing where we’d land.  
“Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
a kind soul we met did demand.  
We heard you speak in the park one day,  
And *māyā* was literally crushed.  
“Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
A voice spoke and our attachment was hushed.  
*Saṅkīrtana* and book distribution  
throughout the day and night.  
“Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
Our devotion reached great heights.  
Devotees joined in huge numbers.  
Many youths really wanted a chance  
to “Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
and take to your process of chant and dance.  
We thought you’d stay with us forever;  
Your personal association was so sweet.  
But we had to “Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
In separation we worshiped your lotus feet.  
“Carry on the legacy; cooperate to spread  
Kṛṣṇa consciousness in your land.”  
“Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
The future is in our hand.  
For ten thousand years to come  
your books the law books will be.  
“Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
This will be the key.  
Ante nārāyaṇa-smṛtiḥ.  
*Bhagavad-gītā* says it all.  
“Think of Kṛṣṇa! Go to Prabhupāda!”  
The writing’s on the wall.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in New Dvārakā, Los Angeles, California, USA.  
(written by Sura Dāsa)

## New Gokula (Vancouver)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, the devotees of New Gokula Dhāma (ISKCON Vancouver) wish to acknowledge their great fortune to be servants at your lotus feet. The following poem, “Śrīla Prabhupāda—Your Loving Ways,” expresses their feelings:

*Śrī*la Prabhupāda—Your Loving Ways

You followed your guru,  
And we follow you.  
Without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
We would not know Kṛṣṇa, the Absolute Truth.  
We swing Him on swings,  
He dances on our tongues,  
We eat His Mahā remnants,  
And chant in *kīrtan.*  
That blue cowherd boy  
Who you love so much,  
By your causeless mercy  
Is now known to us.  
Happiness and joy  
Accompany our days;  
Love and devotion  
Decorate our ways.  
What words can we express?  
What can we say?  
We love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
For your giving ways.  
Selflessly you engaged us,  
Total strangers from the start.  
You melted our steel-clad hearts,  
Which were dirty and dark.  
You saw within us the soul’s tiny spark,  
Which you carefully fanned into flames.  
And now we sing, dance, and chant  
Kṛṣṇa’s transcendental holy names.  
We will do all of this  
Till the end of our days,  
And then we will meet you,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, face to face.  
Thank you, Your Divine Grace.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

With love from  
The devotees at New Gokula Dhāma, Vancouver, Canada.  
(written by Padyāvalī Devī Dāsī)

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day, we bow at your lotus feet and offer our heartfelt, humble obeisances.

Śrīpāda Nayanānanda Bābājī related the following anecdote:

The last year that our gurudeva \[Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura\] organized and performed Navadvīpa-dhāma *Parikramā*, thousands of pilgrims assembled at our Cāṅpāhāṭi temple. There was an entire village of tents stretching in all directions. On the afternoon of the last day, one of our godbrothers had just arrived in Navadvīpa, recently come from London by ship. He had gone there to preach but had returned without meeting full success. That evening during his lecture, Guru-mahārāja explained his earnest desire that Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s teachings be spread in the Western countries. He said that this was the last request of his mother, Śrīmatī Bhagavatī-devī, to him before she left this world, and that indeed it would happen. Consequently, he had been willing to take the lifeblood of the *Gauḍīya* Maṭha funds to send devotees there, yet their attempts had been largely unsuccessful.

At that point in his talk I noticed something mysterious happening. Guru-mahārāja had been looking out at the packed crowd of devotees, especially in the front, where all the *sannyāsīs* and *brahmacārīs* in red cloth were. Then he turned his head and looked over to his left side, where I was standing. He was looking at someone and became silent for some time. I happened to look behind me and saw that the person with whom he was making eye contact was Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Prabhu. I felt that they were looking at each other in a special way. Then Guru-mahārāja turned and again addressed the audience in front of him and said, “But I have a prediction. However long in the future it may be, one of my disciples will cross the ocean and bring back the entire world.” \[Śrī *Bhaktisiddhānta* Vaibhava, Vol. 2, pp. 392–93\]

At your very first meeting with Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī, without even any formalities of exchanging introductions, etc., he expressed his desire that you preach to the English-speaking world.

We are immensely grateful to him for sending you to rescue all of us, and we are immensely grateful to you for spending your life preparing to carry out that request, and then actually doing it. Indeed, across the entire world devotees are engaged in distributing your message. How the entire disciplic succession must rejoice in your triumph!

We stand on the shore of that vast, unfathomable ocean of *bhakti* that you have selflessly delivered to us. And although we have experienced but a drop, we know that it is very deep. All of us want to be better devotees, to utilize our precious time for advancing in loving devotional service. To perfect our lives. But some of us are so distracted by our self-imposed material obligations and the external degrading influences of our environment that we feel lethargic and uninspired. Yet we know the solution. But knowing doesn’t make a big difference.

More than anything, now more than ever, we need your illuminating guidance to apply ourselves to the simple and sublime process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, with all its variety of services. Let us continually hear from you by reading your books and listening to your recorded words. May we chant the holy name purely. We also pray to be empowered by you to spread the rays of the benediction moon, as per your *gurudeva’s* prediction.

With deep gratitude,

Your eternal servants at New Goloka Dhāma, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.  
(written by Kamalinī Devī Dāsī)

## New Govardhana (Australia)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you.

On this auspicious day of your appearance, on behalf of the New Govardhana community, I would like to thank you for all you have done for us, known or unknown.

The world is being shaken by natural disasters—floods, tsunamis, earthquakes—as well as manmade disasters like terrorism. Had you not come, would the world have been taken over by aliens? Or ruined by natural catastrophes? Perhaps by now the drunks in the Bowery would have set the standards of behavior for everyone.

It appears that we have made progress in our behavior as humans: we are not hippielike anymore, more empathy is being taught, tendencies are moving toward saving the planet, and we seem to have the ability to recover from major catastrophes. But who has set the trends? Who has made the human race less animalistic and more inclined toward sattvic activities and respect for one another? What is it that has prevented a force-5 cyclone from wiping out a total state, as it was meant to?

Is it Facebook technology? Better work ethics? Is it science, which blindly reacts to mother nature’s hard blows? Is it the evolution of the species, or perhaps the green movement?

I dare say it is none of the above. It is simply Your Divine Grace’s teachings; the chanting of the holy name you have spread all over the world; the distribution of millions of plates of *kṛṣṇa*-*prasādam;* all the temples you have established as bridges from the material plane to Kṛṣṇa’s abode; all of Kṛṣṇa’s devotees you have made, regardless of their previous sinful activities—devotees who are daily chanting Kṛṣṇa’s glories; all the festivals celebrated, as you taught us. All of this creates the countercurrent to impious activities in this Age of Kali.

It is certain that without you the world would now be a lot worse and Kali would have stepped in a lot faster. But you, as strong as Parīkṣit Mahārāja, drew the sword of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and chastised Kali and set him back ten thousand years.

But as usual, rampant impersonalism has robbed everyone of the intelligence to give Kṛṣṇa and you, His representative, the credit for all those wonderful achievements.

You once said:

If a man makes an artificial imitation of nature, say by barking, people go to see and even pay money. When it comes to the natural barking, no one cares. And when these big so-called scientific rascals claim they can manufacture life, people give all sorts of praise and awards. As for God’s perfect, natural process—millions and millions of beings born at each moment—no one cares. People don’t give God’s process very much credit.

Let us gather today and praise you, giving you due credit by honoring your achievements in defeating Kali and his agents. Please bless us with the ability to serve and honor you rightly as we undertake this challenging and unique task of crediting Kṛṣṇa and his envoy.

Hoping this meets with our approval, we remain  
Your servants at the New Govardhana farm community in Australia.

## New Govardhana (United States)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You taught us that the spiritual master is very merciful because he gives the best possible boon—devotional service—to his disciples. Because of your loving kindness, we are receiving that gift in great measure here in New Govardhana. Out of gratitude for this great gift, we pray that you will allow us to increase our service to you.

In 1961, on the Vyāsa-pūjā day of your spiritual master, you advised your godbrothers that one who serves the guru’s message really worships him. In defining your message, and the message of your guru, you asked your followers to accept the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who said to the *brāhmaṇa* Kūrma: “Tell everyone you meet about Kṛṣṇa, and by My command become a guru and save this land.” This, you said, was your mission.

We are so very grateful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your instructions and for the mercy you give us as we attempt to serve your mission. By the grace of sincere devotees in your New Govardhana community, we are spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in San Diego. Your Krishna Lounge program, 16 Rounds newspaper, Sunday KrishnaFest, and Bhakti Śāstrī course continue to attract new people to the practice of *bhakti-yoga*.

In 1961, you told your godbrothers:

Act for the welfare of others. This is the storehouse of happiness both now and after death. So much work has fallen into your hands. Everyone should join together and do something about it. The world is simply insurmountable *māyā*. If there were a kingdom of Viṣṇu and Vaiṣṇavas on earth, then everyone would be happy.

Your devotees in New Govardhana are making sincere efforts to act for the welfare of others, as you instructed. By giving shelter to new devotees in your *āśrama* and through your Bhakti Chakra and Bhakti *Śikṣā* programs, we get the priceless benediction of participating in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. Through the efforts of sincere book distributors, teachers, lecturers, mentors, and dedicated *pūjārīs*, and through loving exchanges between friends, new devotees become more and more engaged in the precious gift of devotional service.

By cooperating to serve you, we find that we are part of a family of devotees. We find that everyone has something meaningful to offer you, from the senior leaders to the brand-new *bhaktas* and *bhaktins*. We see that your young *brahmacārīs* can take shelter of good instructions from your senior *gṛhasthas* and that your *gṛhasthas* can become enlivened by the enthusiasm and sincere practice of your young *brahmacārīs*. We see that the newest devotees can be the most fired-up preachers of your message, decorating their friends with *tilaka* and bringing them to worship Lord Jagannātha.

Most of all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we see that you empower our tiniest efforts to serve your message, making participation in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission both successful and sublime. We pray that you will enable us to increase our cooperation in your service, to increase the number of devotees in New Govardhana, to increase our commitment to Kṛṣṇa’s service, and to increase our love for you.

Your aspiring servants in New Govardhana Dhāma, San Diego, California, USA.

## New Navadvīpa

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace and your sincere followers.

As elsewhere, most of your present followers in Hawaii never saw you walk this earth. When I tell them what it was like to see you, they always want to hear more. So to please those who are pleasing you, I will describe what it was like for me to see you for the first time.

By July 1971, I had been reading your books and listening to your voice for about a year. On Friday the 16th we arrived in Detroit from New Vrindaban, where I had found my Kṛṣṇa consciousness finally “taking root” after joining your movement a year earlier in Boston. You had been away from America that year and had recently returned to Los Angeles, the first stop on a cross-country tour in which you were initiating the hundreds of devotees joining your “Hare Kṛṣṇa explosion.”

From New Vrindaban; Ontario, Canada; and the American Midwest we trekked, descending on Detroit’s Metro Airport in a way unthinkable today. *Ching-ching-ching,* *boom-boom-boom* resounded our *kīrtana,* transforming the airport and beckoning your late-arriving jet. Even after it landed, you must have been the last one off the plane, the *kīrtana* building to a nearly unbearable crescendo until at last you appeared.

I couldn’t see you, but I knew you must be there because—left, right, and center—whole swaths of devotees were cascading to the floor, dumbstruck by what they saw. And then it happened to me. “Absolute power flowing through absolute humility,” I later reflected. You don’t see that in the material world. But if you do, the soul spontaneously responds. Total joy tackles you and down you go. *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Floating atop a sea of devotees, you made your way across the gate’s lounge to a makeshift, canopied *vyāsāsana,* where you sang Hare Kṛṣṇa and surveyed the new faces riding the *saṅkīrtana* wave.
