Holding high a silver chalice, you air-dropped a steady stream of water into your lotus mouth. When someone presented a tray of sweets, you tossed one through full lips and flicked in an errant crumb with your *surabhi* tongue. “*Jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda paramahaṁsa* . . .” you commanded, sending us to the floor again. Then you spoke.

It has become very satisfactory that so many devotees, boys and girls, are taking part in this great movement, Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. It is very important movement because it is correcting the human civilization. There is a great defect in the modern civilization, and people are accepting this body as self, and based on this mistake in the foundation, everything is going wrong. The basic principle of civilization—accepting this body as the soul—is the beginning of all problems.

Mentioning your recent Moscow talks with Professor Kotovsky, you continued the same message, a simple message, but to hear you say it with such profound truth and beauty elicited the highest praise I knew: WOW!

So basic principle of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is to correct the wrong foundation of the human civilization. In the *Bhagavad-gītā* this is the beginning of spiritual knowledge. When Arjuna was identifying himself with this body and bodily relationship, Kṛṣṇa first of all corrected that “You are not this body.” So if we do not understand these first steps of spiritual knowledge, then where is the question of making further progress?

Suddenly, from the public address system boomed the airport’s own concern about progress: “Ladies and gentlemen, I have to ask you to clear the boarding area. We have a flight leaving here in about thirty minutes. We have to check it in. So if you’ll kindly clear the boarding area, please.” Your Divine Grace was the first to comply.

Your business finished, you led us back to the temple, where the following day you kindly initiated me. But that’s another story, Śrīla Prabhupāda, one your younger followers also ask about. And why not? You may be my initiating spiritual master, but in ISKCON you are everyone’s founder-*ācārya*. We are your *anugas,* your instructed followers, and to enhance our common, core kinship, I beg your permission to go on sharing my little “Prabhupāda stories.” For aging eyewitnesses like me, what better service, what greater fortune? Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants in your New Navadvīpa community, Honolulu, Hawaii, USA,  
(written by Sureśvara Dāsa)

## New Ramaṇ Reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

On this most glorious occasion of your appearance, we are eager to present our offerings unto you for your pleasure. These offerings are the combined efforts of many hundreds of devotees in the New Ramaṇ Reti community, and they encompass many devotional and preaching activities.

Last year we were able to install Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma in Their temple here at New Ramaṇ Reti. This was done with the help of more than four hundred families and individuals who came forward in large and small ways to make their contribution to serve Their Lordships. Seamstresses completed the sewing on many new outfits so that Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma would have new outfits matching the many outfits already in use by Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. These outfits were graced with all new jewelry, ornaments, and crowns. Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma stand on a hand-carved base on an exquisitely carved *siṁhāsana* on Their beautiful altar. To properly store all Their dresses and paraphernalia, the *pūjārī* room was expanded, and many cabinets and closets were installed there by dedicated devotees who wanted to see that you had a most excellent facility for serving Their Lordships.

Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma were then installed on Their altar and worshiped in an unforgettable festival. The Deity installation was performed by many *brāhmaṇas* and senior devotees. The elaborate procedures were absorbing for everyone and done with great expertise. The two-day festival had continuous ecstatic *kīrtanas,* and the *prasādam* seemed to overflow continuously.

To facilitate future ever-expanding plans to prepare huge quantities of *prasādam,* we had a new, modern kitchen built. It is well equipped and professionally designed. It is truly a most wonderful facility. The Deities have Their own separate kitchen, so all Their daily offerings can be easily and expertly prepared.

All our feasts are served outdoors, and for years the guests and devotees often had to sit out in the open air. Again the devotees and congregation came together to construct a *prasādam* pavilion, which will shelter everyone from the sun and rain. It also offers a nice facility for dramas and other performances.

Mothers Laxmimonī and Ākūti have created the Bhakti Bhavan, a *bhaktin* training center. There they hold many training seminars and teach Bhakti Śāstrī courses, which facilitate young women who want to learn the details of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Book distribution, which is so dear to your heart, has increased. Many books were distributed during the very successful December marathon because of a united effort by the community of devotees. The devotees are very inspired to serve you in this way, and many of them were personally involved in the distribution.

*Prasādam* distribution continued to expand this past year, as we increased the number of persons served daily on the campus of Santa Fe College.

Many devotees helped the Ratha-yātrā team, headed by Bhadrā and Dharmarāja Prabhus, put on Ratha-yātrās in seven cities across North Florida. These events were attended by over 100,000 visitors and hundreds of devotees.

New Ramaṇ Reti is situated on a 127 acres of land. On that land our Save the Cow team continues to take excellent care of your cows. We also have expanded our vegetable gardens, which supply the Deities with organic produce year round.

A Vaiṣṇava Care team, which cares for elderly and terminally ill devotees, has been further expanded.

The second-generation devotees have continued to make our festivals extraordinary, putting on dramas, creating a Village of Vrindavan exhibit of holy places, and performing many other services. These festivals attract thousands of devotees and congregation members, including our expanding Indian congregation, who come from all over North Florida.

The second generation is also taking major responsibility for teaching in the Bhaktivedanta Academy, which trains our children.

We hope that these offerings are pleasing to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. They represent the combined efforts of the devotees in the temple community.

We continue to beg for your mercy so that we can continue to expand the glories of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa and bring more and more conditioned souls to surrender at your lotus feet.

*Your humble servants at the New Rama*ṇ Reti rural community in Alachua, Florida, USA

## New Tālavana Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance. All glories to your divine, transcendental loving devotional service. You are tasting the nectar of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, and we are very happy. Please allow us to continue serving your transcendental orders and attempting to fulfill your sublime desires, so that we may achieve shelter at your transcendental lotus feet.

Are we actually in touch with you? Are other members of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness in touch with you? Certainly the answer can be found in *śāstra,* or by speaking with advanced devotees. We have learned from you that the *vāṇī* (the message) of the spiritual master is greater then the *vapu* (form), or in other words that the instructions of the guru is more important than his physical presence. Consequently we can always be in touch with the spiritual master through his instructions. Surely his physical presence is spiritually enlivening, but his instructions are with us forever. They give us his guidance, his sweet loving words, and the transcendental perspective.

Therefore, according to our degree of comprehension and following of your instructions, we are in touch with you. Is there any doubt about that? Let us assume there is some doubt. Can it be dispelled? Is it so strong that it creates a negative mode in our devotional service and keeps us from dispelling it? Let us assume it is that heavy. Still, it absolutely can be dispelled by the power of devotional service. But we’ve assumed we cannot do it. What is not possible for us is possible for the spiritual master, *sādhus,* *śāstras,* and Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Devotional service begins with this faith.

By good fortune one becomes eligible to cross the ocean of nescience, and when one’s term of material existence decreases, one may get an opportunity to associate with pure devotees. By such association, one’s attraction to Kṛṣṇa is awakened. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.45\]

By associating with a devotee, one awakens his faith in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.49\]

Please forgive me, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for insufficiently quoting scriptural references to prove my logic. Certainly I can do the research, but I prefer to go on, and if time permits I will add more. My point is that any devotee can stay in touch with you even though you are not physically present. Your touch is our desired object. All humans are motivated by an objective. Unfortunately, most are motivated by temporary and very limited objectives pertaining only to this world. A few, with some conception of transcendence, desire liberation or mystic powers. Our objective is your service, because that service is the best welfare for all living entities and gives us your association. You have so nicely explained everything concerning transcendental knowledge that we unequivocally want your association and service forever. Factually your explanations of Vedic knowledge can deliver the hopeless conditioned souls throughout the universe and keep us in connection with you.

It is your arrangements that have brought us to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It is your devotional service and your words—your logical arguments, scriptural references, and explanations—that have changed our thinking and given us the desire for God, or Kṛṣṇa consciousness. *Yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo:* by the spiritual master’s mercy, the disciple gains Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. You taught us this prayer by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura (*Gurv-aṣṭaka* 8), and you showed us the truth of this prayer by your personal example of unflinching service to your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

Writing, producing, and distributing your translations of the most important Vedic literatures for mankind certainly constitute your most important devotional service. It is through these great literatures and the transcendental devotional service they teach that we and others can continue to associate with you, clear our hearts of doubts and delusion, become more and more Kṛṣṇa conscious, and ultimately join you and the Lord. Your divine mercy makes it possible for us to be one in interest with Śrīman Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement and to take guidance from God within.

Kṛṣṇa is situated in everyone’s heart as the *caittya-guru,* the spiritual master within. When He is kind to some fortunate conditioned soul, He personally gives him lessons so he can progress in devotional service, instructing the person as the Supersoul within and the spiritual master without. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.47\]

Some people are skeptical when they hear someone claiming to directly take God’s guidance, since there are so many fools and rascals who make similar claims. But taking such guidance is the natural way of life for devotees, and it is very practical. Everything is easily verifiable by consulting the scriptures you have given us, your words and those of the previous *ācāryas,* and the instructions of saintly sādhus or holy men. Materialists spend thousands of hours researching their papers, plans, and books, but you have taught us how to get in touch with God very quickly and easily, and we do not need modern technology or libraries. We’ve learned to contact the Absolute Truth simply through His sound incarnation. You are the connection, and you’ve given us one simple mantra to do the trick, giving us results that are far beyond what *karmīs,* *jñānīs,* and *yogīs* can attain even after dedicating massive amounts of time, facilities, and endeavor. Surely we owe you more then our very life.

O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the *ācārya* and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 11.29.6\]

You are so generous that you have given us the same transcendental life you lived, breathed, and spoke about. We are endeavoring to follow your teachings and example, Śrīla Prabhupāda. When I look around at your dear granddisciples here at New Tālavana, I see them growing up into young transcendentalists eager to lead Kṛṣṇa conscious lives. All of them who are following your teachings I see as happy as transcendental cowherd people. Yes, you have done the wonder you so many times referred to while convincing others of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. It is still going on and getting better and stronger. Sure there are still calamities, but that is part of living within the material universe. You have taught and showed us how to deal with reverses. You have empowered us to call upon the name of God, not to fulfill our petty material desires but to carry on the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We cannot adequately express our thankfulness. It is simply wondrous that we are gaining more and more fulfillment by carrying out your order to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all. When we try to do so, contact with you becomes a reality. Yes, the shade of your lotus feet is our most valuable object, and that is our objective.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this opportunity to link with you and the Supreme Personality of Godhead, His devotees, and His mission. Thank you for remaining our ever well-wisher and allowing us to continue serving your lotus feet, birth after birth. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your ISKCON, your BBT, your farms, and everything else you’ve given us. All glories to your devotional service!

Sincerely,  
Your disciples and granddisciples at New Tālavana Dhāma, Mississippi, USA.  
*(*written by Yogīndra Vandana Dāsa)

## New Varṣāṇā (New Zealand)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

During my visit to the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara Temple in Śrī Vṛndāvana this year, I happened to come across a realization you wrote that entered deep into my heart. So this year I would like to make your realization my offering, for I feel it to be one of the most amazing things I have ever read—your personal realization, written in your room in the hub of the spiritual world. It is full of great spiritual insight and gives hints to all as to how through advancement in spiritual life one can have the association of the great *ācāryas* and can also be with you eternally.

Those who stay there \[at the Rādhā-Dāmodara Temple\] will realize that those *ācāryas* \[Śrīla Rūpa *Gosvāmī*, Śrīla Jīva *Gosvāmī*, Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja *Gosvāmī*, and Śrīla Bhūgarbha *Gosvāmī*\] are still living there. And if one is actually advancing in the spiritual field, one can still take instruction from the *ācāryas* living there. It is up to the individual how he realizes this by his devotional service. By staying there you must realize how pure the environment is. The environment itself gives a high taste of consciousness. Rādhā-Dāmodara are there, and the great *ācāryas’* *samādhis* are there. There cannot be any other better place to live than Rādhā-Dāmodara temple. This corner of the Rādhā-Dāmodara Temple is just like the hub of the wheel of the spiritual world. It is the center. I eternally reside in my rooms at the Rādhā-Dāmodara Temple.

I personally cannot find anything greater than to have your eternal association.

Your servants at New Varṣāṇā, ISKCON’s rural farm community in New Zealand.  
(written by Kalasaṁvara Dāsa)

## New Vraja Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

The mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is to spread the only medicine effective in this sinful Age of Kali: the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. He predicted that one day the holy name would be chanted everywhere:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*

**“In every town and village, the chanting of My name will be heard.” (*****Caitanya-bhāgavata,*** ***Antya*** **4.126)**

Lord Caitanya could have spread *harināma* *saṅkīrtana* all over the world Himself, but He left this special mission to you because He takes more pleasure when His devotees are glorified. It was the strong desire of Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura that the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu be spread to every country of the Western world. Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī therefore gave this instruction to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western countries to anyone who displayed some intelligence and spoke English. This was the first instruction that he gave you, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, and you were the only disciple among the many who received it who really took his instruction seriously. You left Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma and came to the West, taking on a lot of risks and austerities to fulfill the desire of your beloved spiritual master.

Vāsudeva Datta, a very intimate associate of Lord Caitanya, prayed to the Lord in this way:

*jīvera duḥkha dekhi’ mora hṛdaya bidare*  
*sarva-jīvera pāpa prabhu deha’ mora śire*

***“*****My Lord, my heart breaks to see the sufferings of all the conditioned souls; therefore I request You to transfer the karma of their sinful lives upon my head.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya*** **15.162)**

Reflecting Vāsudeva Datta’s deep sympathy and humble mood, you wrote onboard the *Jaladuta*, in your “Prayer to the Lotus Feet of Kṛṣṇa”:

“When all the sinful, miserable living entities become happy, the Vaiṣṇavas’ desire is then fulfilled.”

And then a few days later, in your “*Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma*” poem, you prayed to Lord Kṛṣṇa when you arrived in Boston:

“If You so desire, I wish that You may deliver them.”

By your personal example you showed the attitude of a perfect Vaiṣṇava, someone who is ready to accept suffering to give the opportunity for others to go back to Godhead.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the day of your appearance is the most auspicious celebration for us because you brought us the most valuable gift to save us: the original, pure teachings of Lord Caitanya. With a grateful heart we thank you. With your books and lectures you taught the entire world and presented the spiritual practices that are able to elevate anyone to the platform of pure devotion. By your serious efforts and great sacrifice you established numerous temples, preaching centers, Govinda’s restaurants, and farm communities around the world, and with your pure and effulgent personality you attracted many thousands of people to join ISKCON.

You taught that farm communities provide a good opportunity for devotees to live a simple Kṛṣṇa-conscious life, a life based on farming and cow protection, with Kṛṣṇa’s service at the center. Following the principles you set out, devotees under the leadership of your beloved disciple His Holiness Śivarāma Swami established a farm community here in Hungary eighteen years ago, naming it New Vraja-dhāma. A few years later, in 1996, our beloved Deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara, came to us to mercifully accept the daily worship and devotion of Their devotees. All this was possible by your mercy and by the mercy of your wonderful disciples, for which we are eternally grateful and indebted to you.

In the last eighteen years many aspects of New Vraja-dhāma have gradually unfolded due to the significant efforts of your disciples and granddisciples. First, the farm community has moved more and more toward self-sufficiency centered on protecting cows and engaging oxen in farming, all so that we can offer Rādhe-Śyāma tasty preparations made from homegrown vegetables, fruits, and grain, as well as milk from Their own cows. In this way we would like to set an example of “simple living and high thinking.” In addition, New Vraja-dhāma is a wonderful holy place, the ever-beautiful realm of Rādhe-Śyāma, where tens of thousands of tourists and many devotees visit to get the *darśana* of the most enchanting Rādhe-Śyāma, taste the sweetness of the holy name, and become filled with the devotional mood that pervades here. And above all, New Vraja-dhāma is the spiritual home of our Kṛṣṇa-conscious community, where our children take birth and grow up in an ideal spiritual atmosphere, where the loving care of devotees grows, and where the service of Rādhe-Śyāma and the Vaiṣṇavas is in the center of devotees’ hearts. Our greatest treasure is the association of the Lord’s devotees, and so we gratefully thank you and your disciples for allowing us to live in such a nice Vaiṣṇava society.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the purpose of all our endeavors is to please you and the *guru-paramparā*. We can express our love and gratitude toward you best if we cooperate in the service of your mission. On behalf of our community, I would like to offer all our efforts and activities and service at your lotus feet and humbly ask for your mercy so that we can assist one another in the service of our spiritual master in a selfless mood and cooperative spirit, all aimed at realizing your transcendental vision of *daivī*-*varṇāśrama*.

Your servants at New Vraja-dhāma, Hungary.

## New Vrindavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this glorious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, please accept our most humble obeisances.

For ten thousand more years, ISKCON, the *saṅkīrtana* movement of which you are the founder-*ācārya,* will be the vehicle by which to cross the dangerous shark-infested ocean of Kali-yuga, in which the fearful people of the world are at present drowning without relief. In view of this astounding fact, can there be a more auspicious day for the conditioned souls than the anniversary of your appearance? Can there be a gratitude more prescient than acknowledging our exceeding good fortune in becoming your disciples and granddisciples during this brief window of opportunity in earthly affairs, after which time the thorough degradation of the human spirit will become a foregone conclusion?

Choosing America to begin your movement because of its leading and exemplary position among the nations of the world, you came here and gave yourself cent percent to anyone who had the slightest inclination to hear your divine message. Thus you sowed the seeds of their redemption by empowering them to forego their mostly degenerate lifestyles. In this way you not only brought relief to their conflicted and bewildered minds, but you saved them from the tragic circumstances of conditioned life—namely, the repetition of birth and death. How could you have voluntarily put yourself in that unenviable position night and day unless you were a pure lover of Lord Kṛṣṇa and felt the true compassion for His parts and parcels that is a quality of a pure unalloyed devotee?

You warned of the dire consequences of thinking that the acquisition of money will solve our devotional problems.

You warned of the danger of relegating our main concern for ourselves and others to that of physical well-being and comfort.

You told us that the unregulated free mixing of the sexes was detrimental to spiritual life, as also the uncontrolled wagging of our tongues.

You told us explicitly what to accept and what to reject; indeed, you taught us everything required to protect us from the pernicious effects of the bodily concept of life.

You brought the very welcome news that there was another world in which we should be freely manifesting our affections, a world of knowledge and bliss, a world without toil and extinction. You explained the circumstances which would make that home of Lord Kṛṣṇa our rightful inheritance.

If we have failed thus far in clearly establishing for ourselves the goals and practices that you so mercifully implored us to adopt, it can be for no other reason than that we have not taken your instructions sufficiently to heart, that we have not yet made them the most important and pressing aspect of our lives. What simpler remedy for all our shortcomings and confusions than for us communally to establish ourselves in this empowering aspect of devotional life?

We are not simply surmising that it was your dream that communities like New Vrindaban become exemplary in pure spiritual living, because you wrote as much. A perfect community practical for all types of people and based on brahminical culture was what you envisaged, a community dependent on agriculture and cows as a means of livelihood.

So if we chant and worship, and till and sow and reap, and care for the cows, and educate our children in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, as was your fond desire for the residents of New Vrindaban, we will without doubt experience the benefits derived from pleasing you. Thus we will experience a new happiness transcendental to any to be experienced endeavoring to satisfy selfish yearnings. As you assured us on so many occasions, it will be a joyous undertaking without limit, and also without the inherent deficiencies of the purely materialistic way of life.

In our fidelity to you we will of course also be pleasing their Lordships Rādhā-Vrindāvan Chandra and the rest of the Deities, whose presence here we accept as being the result of the causeless mercy that you have bestowed, mercy we trust you will continue to bestow upon your surrendered servants through the everlasting medium of your *vāṇī*. Thus in the ultimate satisfaction of Lord Kṛṣṇa we will achieve the perfection of our lives and establish fully the miracle of your invaluable association.

We remain eternally indebted and thankful.

Your humble servants at New Vrindaban Dhāma, West Virginia, USA.

## New York (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We come together on this most blessed occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā as insignificant beggars lacking adequate devotional qualities to qualify for the ultimate causeless mercy of Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī* and Śrī Kṛṣṇa—love of God, *kṛṣṇa-prema*. We simply bow down and stand before you, at your transcendental lotus feet, our hands held out, hankering for your mercy, eagerly awaiting your love, your inspirational acknowledgement of our eternal relationship, our hearts blossoming with humility, hoping, praying, and shamelessly begging for a Kṛṣṇa Cookie of your divine mercy, love, and elevation to the sincere, steady, and highest spiritual platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the prime benediction for every living entity.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu provides the recipe to prepare those delicious Kṛṣṇa Cookies of mercy and love that Your Divine Grace so fondly likes to feed us, your insignificant children:

Glories to the *śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana,* which cleanses the heart of all the dust accumulated for years together. Thus the fire of conditioned life, of repeated birth and death, is extinguished. This *saṅkīrtana* movement is the prime benediction for humanity at large because it spreads the rays of benediction moon. It is the life of all transcendental knowledge, it increases the ocean of transcendental bliss, and it helps to *have* a taste of the full nectar for which we are always anxious. \[*Śrī* *Śikṣāṣṭaka* 1\]

Bowing down and standing before Your Divine Grace, seated regally and appearing beautifully on your *vyāsāsana,* our eternal father, our eternal master, and our ever well-wishing friend, we become childlike servants in your glorious presence. You bring out the best in all of us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Every devotee wants to offer you his or her love and devotion, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please help us. We are drowning in an ocean of sorrow, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your association enriches us beyond the limits of our present spiritual advancement. Your association empowers us, and it enables us to serve and surrender to your guidance, teaching us rapidly with each act of sincere devotional service our great responsibility and the great need to be kind and share your mercy and love with other, less fortunate and unfortunate parts and parcels of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrīla Govindadeva, the Supreme lovers and friends of the wonderful Brijabāsīs, the eternal residents of Śrī Vṛndāvana Dhāma.

We become humble, kind, loving servants and friends as we receive matchless gifts of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that you offer to all of us who want to serve you with love and devotion, and even to those of us who stand before you, insignificant fools like me, shameless beggars at your lotus feet. We congregate at your divine lotus feet, our empty hands and hankering hearts just waiting for you to notice us, to acknowledge our eternal relationship, waiting to see when these hard, cold hankering hearts will become sincere, meek, and humble, thus qualifying us for one of those priceless Kṛṣṇa Cookies, which nourish and fill our hearts and souls with the bliss of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and deepen our fortunate attachment and dedication to serving your mission.

We need your mercy and special blessings, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are in darkness, we cannot see, we are blind prisoners, victims of *Māyā*’s illusions. Please help us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are fully dependent on your mercy and love. Especially today, on your Vyāsa-pūjā day, your appearance day, we beg on behalf of all Kṛṣṇa consciousness practitioners, all friends of this great movement, the International Society for Krishna consciousness, Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s movement—we beg Your Divine Grace again and again, and yet again, for one of your special Kṛṣṇa Cookies of mercy so we may clean off the dust of *Māyā* covering our consciousness and polluting our hearts. We beg Your Divine Grace for causeless, pure devotional service in the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, birth after birth.

We beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for a little bit of your all-powerful mercy so we will surrender our life, our hearts, and our souls in pure devotion to Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the Golden Avatar, who has a heart of gold which is the repository of an unlimited ocean of eternal love and devotion available for everyone who comes in contact with the free and causeless devotional service Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi distribute in the marketplace of the holy name. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has planted the seeds to transform this Iron Age of Kali into the Golden Age of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare *Rāma,* Hare *Rāma,* *Rāma* *Rāma,* Hare Hare. Now if we act as meek and humble assistants and water those seeds of causeless devotion planted by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, we are certain that Your Divine Grace will smile upon us with blessings and that Lord Gaurāṅga will lovingly embrace us to His golden heart of eternal bliss and love.

The great Vaiṣṇava *ācārya* Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura surely knows the revealed path leading to the blessing of mercy and love from Your Divine Grace:

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. Therefore, I should always remember and praise the spiritual master. At least three times a day I should offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, more of us in our assemblies need and must seek the path of full surrender to your purifying *śikṣā*-*vāṇī,* your Kṛṣṇa conscious directions. All who do and who dare to become attached to hearing and serving Your Divine Grace in the form of that spiritually superpotent *śikṣā*-*vāṇī* will quickly forget all their miseries, which are deep and dangerous like an ocean of birth and death. Those who surrender will become free from birth and death, sheltered on Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s spiritual ship, the ship which you captain, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on the peaceful, blissful, and loving ocean of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s and Śrīla Govindadeva’s ocean of the nectar of pure devotion.

“He reasons ill who says that Vaiṣṇavas die,  
when thou art living still in sound.  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live and, living, try  
to spread the holy name around.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need your love. Please give all of us more of those superdelicious Kṛṣṇa Cookies directly from your divine lotuslike fingers, frosted with love and friendship from your pure Kṛṣṇa conscious heart. We hanker for one of those Kṛṣṇa Cookies and may dry up and die without one.

Your unfortunate, insignificant servants and beggars at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva Mandir, Brooklyn, New York, USA.  
(written by Rāmabhadra Dāsa)

## Noida

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this very memorable day, on behalf of ISKCON Noida and its congregation, we offer our *daṇḍavat* *pranāmas* to your lotus feet. Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, and all glories to your appearance on this planet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as you know, ISKCON Noida is dedicated to your divine teachings and is known formally as The Bhaktivedanta Academy for Spiritual Sciences (BASS). By your mercy we are making a little progress. Whenever we organize educational seminars, especially courses on various topics of Vaiṣṇava philosophy, they attract lots of people, and the people’s appreciation reminds us that you have given this material world a matchless gift. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this special day I would like to give you a brief report, particularly on our preaching to the youth—how they are coming in contact with your great movement here at the Noida ISKCON center.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as you said, youth is a special blessing of the Lord, a time when one is full of energy, quite receptive to new ideas, and relatively free of social responsibilities. A young person can easily be taught to practice the path of perfection and easily imbibe a genuine service attitude toward humanity.

In this fast-growing tech-savvy India, cities like Noida have become technology hubs in terms of high-end education and employment. From all parts of India, large numbers of youths not yet even eighteen come to Noida for training in information technology (IT) and management, as well as to find lucrative jobs. At such an age, they find themselves far from the guidance and shelter of their respected parents and relatives, who to one degree or another had tried to inculcate them with good culture and moral values. In big cities like Noida, where the influence of Kali-yuga is strongest, these tender-aged youths easily fall victim to bad influences and begin indulging in nefarious activities like intoxication, illicit sex, and meat-eating.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the leaders of our youth preaching, well aware of the above facts, are convinced that the remedy for all sorts of problems is Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and that the best and easiest way for one to become Kṛṣṇa conscious is to take shelter of your divine teachings. So our youth leaders are constantly making many kinds programs to attract youths: Saturday Love Feasts, festivals, preaching programs at reputed marketplaces, schools, and colleges, etc. In this way they take every opportunity to bring these youths closer to your great movement, and they are having some success here at Noida ISKCON.

Lord Kṛṣṇa is attractive to people of all ages, but He seems especially appreciated and accepted by young men and women. We have all heard how, when Your Divine Grace started preaching in the USA in 1965, all-attractive and all-youthful Lord Kṛṣṇa very easily captivated the minds of the most bewildered and lost youths of that age, cleansing their hearts of all malice and filling them with love and service.

So, we seek Your Divine Grace’s blessings. Kindly shower your mercy upon us so that we can be inspired to contact, convince, and cultivate new youths for your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bestow your mercy upon all the young people of Noida so they can escape the strong clutches of materialism, accept the path of self-realization, and attain the ultimate goal of life.

Your servants at ISKCON Noida, India.  
(written by Vraja Bhakti Vilāsa Dāsa)

## Pandharpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet on the occasion of the 115th anniversary of your appearance.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are very fortunate to live and serve in your wonderful temple here, right on the bank of Chandrabhaga River, in Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma. Last year was a great source of transcendental joy and ecstatic thrills for us as we engeged in your services.

We have concentrated on youth preaching and congregational Nāmahaṭtas in the areas surrounding Pandharpur, i.e., villages and towns in the districts of Solapur, Osmanabad, and Latur. To encourage college students to study and realize the unique importance of *Śrīmad* *Bhagavad-gītā*—which teaches how to perfect one’s life by dovetailing all one’s actions in the service of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead—we conducted the Bhaktivedanta Talent Search (BTS) Competition in Pandharpur, Osmanabad, and Latur. Eight hundred students from more than thirty colleges took part in the BTS Competition, which was based on the first six chapters of the *Bhagavad-gītā*. The Competition was well received by many college students and their professors, so the prospects for conducting a similar program during the next academic year are high. For Nāmahaṭṭa preaching, the weekly *sat-saṅga* programs and street *harināma* *kīrtanas* followed by *prasādam* distribution and book distribution are being conducted in villages, towns, and districts in the surrounding areas. As a result of this preaching, the number of visitors to the temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pandharinātha has increased. As usual, Food for Life was the main program in our temple here at Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma. The Ekādaśī Festival was observed with great devotion and charm; around 1.5 million pilgrims visited, and 300,000 were served *prasādam* in Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma during the festival period.

We get life and strength by distributing your transcendental books. Our first priority during last December’s marathon was to increase the *saṅkīrtana* scores. Our specific plan was to approach those who had not yet received any of your divine literature, which paves the path back home, back to Godhead. Our *saṅkīrtana* parties thus visited for the first time so many small towns and villages near Pandharpur.

The state government of Maharashtra has appointed the Pandharpur Development Authority (PDA) to oversee the multimillion-dollar project of developing Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma. The aim is to make it a most beautiful pilgrimage place equipped with all facilities for the hundreds of thousands of pilgrims to enjoy the natural spiritual atmosphere here in Bhūvaikuṇṭha. It is here that Mother Chandrabhaga flows so magnificently, providing everyone an opportunity to bathe in her divine waters and cleanse away all sins. By thus receiving the causeless mercy Mother Chandrabhaga, one becomes qualified to touch the transcendental lotus feet of the all-merciful Śrī Śrī Viṭhṭhala-Rukmiṇī.

Out of all the *āśramas* and cultural organizations in Pandharpur, ISKCON of Pandharpur was acccepted by the Pandharpur Development Authority as most suitable to educate the misguided younger generation, cultivate human values in them, and inspire them with true devotion to Lord Viṭhṭhala. The PDA will draw up the blueprints for the Pandharpur Development Project in consultation with ISKCON of Pandharpur. Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma has been a place of pilgrimage since ancient times. We encourage ISKCON temples in Maharashtra and throughout India to bring *yātrās* (spiritual tours) to Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma. Here they can conduct various seminars and engage in *kṛṣṇa-kathā* in the most jubilant and enlivening spiritual atmosphere. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us by giving us the opportunity to repeatedly serve you in Śrī Pandharpur Dhāma, where tears come to our eyes out of love while we serve you!

Your servants at ISKCON Pandharpur, India.

## Paris

Dear beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Kṛṣṇa so wonderfully arranged for you to appear in this world on the day after Janmāṣṭamī; after all, without your transcendental association we could not possibly understand the nature of Kṛṣṇa’s birth and activities in this world.

It is Your Divine Grace who revealed the truth of Kṛṣṇa! Prior to your arrival in the Western world, there was so many translations of the *Bhagavad-gītā* available, but none of them produced even a single devotee of Kṛṣṇa. In France, for centuries scholars had read the *Gītā, but* no one concluded that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Godhead. Your English translation of *Bhagavad-gītā,* which was later translated into dozens of other languages and distributed all over the world, has transformed the hearts of millions of souls, from the lowest *mlecchas* to the topmost Vaiṣṇavas. This is your power, the power of a great personality who is completely surrendered to the loving service of the Lord.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you often said that the greatest service we can render to Lord Kṛṣṇa is to promote His transcendental glories far and wide. You showed this by your own example: out of deep compassion for the fallen conditioned souls, in the mood of *Nityānanda* Prabhu and Śrī *Gaurāṅga* Mahāprabhu, you distributed the mercy of Kṛṣṇa freely to everyone, not discriminating among who was fit or unfit.

When you left Vṛndāvana, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Kṛṣṇa followed you in the form of His holy name. Kṛṣṇa followed you to Bombay, as you sailed on the *Jaladuta* across the Atlantic ocean to America, and as you then traveled to Europe, to Moscow, to South America, to Australia. For thirteen years Kṛṣṇa followed right behind you. You were constantly hearing the tinkling of His ankle bells (which you told us *Śrī* Parisīśvara should always wear), and therefore you had the complete faith needed to fearlessly preach His message to everyone. In the form of the holy name, Kṛṣṇa bore witness to your words, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and anyone who chanted it received the supreme mercy of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave Kṛṣṇa in the form of His holy name and taught us that whoever we are and wherever we are, whenever we sincerely chant the holy name in a mood of humility and service, Kṛṣṇa will be there. *Tṛṇād api sunīcena taror iva sahiṣṇunā/ amāninā mānadena kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ.* If we chant the holy name in a spirit of service, humble like the blade of grass, tolerant like the tree, and ready to offer all respects to others while not expecting any in return, Kṛṣṇa will inspire us to chant eternally.

On this 115th anniversary of your appearance in this world, let us try to understand the glories of your compassion, Śrīla Prabhupāda. If we do so, Kṛṣṇa will surely reveal Himself to us in full. Why? Because your compassion, Śrīla Prabhupāda, embodies the supreme mercy of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara, and to understand your compassion we must follow your instructions, and your main instruction is to always chant the holy name—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Your servants in Paris, France.  
(written by Rāma Dāsa)

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Here in the Perth *mandira* we have been reading once a week from Satsvarūpa Mahārāja’s *Līlāmṛta,* and we are amazed at how much compassion you showed the conditioned souls by leaving Kṛṣṇa’s abode of Vṛndāvana to preach among unregulated, intoxicated, and often disturbed people in the Bowery of New York.

We would like to extend a small portion of your mercy to the people of Perth. Over the last twelve months we have become entangled in a dispute with the local council over our plans to complete your temple here. We do not know what the outcome of this dispute will be, nor do we know Kṛṣṇa’s plan for the Perth *yātrā*. How we would love to offer our obeisances to you, describe the situation to you, and hear your analysis. We could then proceed with such confidence.

We suspect that Kṛṣṇa has a plan in mind beyond our limited comprehension, and on this Vyāsa-pūjā day we pray that you help us all progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that Kṛṣṇa’s plan for the Perth *yātrā* will become manifest to us.

Begging to remain eternally your humble servants,  
Your devotees in Perth, Australia.

## Philippines

**Without A Doubt**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to your blessed appearance day!

There is not the slightest doubt in our minds that you came directly from Kṛṣṇa’s abode and appeared on this earthly planet just to save fallen *jīvas* like ourselves and bring us back to Kṛṣṇa. No other *ācārya* accomplished what you did. And even though you have left our vision, your legacy, the ISKCON movement, is continuing to spread all over the globe. This is due to the spiritual potency of your divine personality. Without a shadow of a doubt history will record that you, by the sublime teachings in your books, saved the whole world. The world today is steadily gliding down to a hellish condition of life. Practically speaking, if we depend solely on material remedies, there is no hope of mitigating the unending miseries all around us. Kali-yuga is rapidly progressing, and spirituality is diminishing. In fact, this is not at all surprising. You have taught us in your books that this would happen. Therefore you very expertly made arrangements so those who wish to seek refuge and ultimately escape the clutches of repeated birth and death can be saved by taking shelter in your movement, ISKCON.

You are without a doubt an empowered representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Thus you are not an ordinary human being like the rest of us. Even so, what makes you very special is your quality of being *patita-pāvana.* Your compassion knows no bounds. Although we are most unworthy of your mercy, you still picked us up from our deluded lifestyle and somehow transformed our consciousness, making us Kṛṣṇa conscious devotees. Because of the love you gave us, we know we should at least attempt to reciprocate. But honestly, our efforts remain futile and even shameful. We can only pray that you kindly accept our endeavor to please you and engage us in devotional service, life after life.

From the bottom of our hearts we sincerely thank you for your kindness, mercy, tolerance, and most of all the love you unconditionally gave us. We can never repay you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. So we request that you kindly make us your humble and menial servants. In this way we hope to eternally serve you and bring many more fallen, conditioned souls to the shelter of your divine lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Vyāsa-pūjā kī jaya!

Your servants at the Philippines yātrā.  
(written by Śrīdāmā Dāsa)

## Phoenix

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

Recently we were recounting your pastimes before coming to America and soon after your arrival. We were amazed at how much you struggled to establish this movement. Before coming to America, while traveling to America, and during your early days here, you encountered so many difficulties. But you remained undaunted in your determination to fulfill the order of your spiritual master. He asked you to print books and propagate Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the West. You wrote the books, printed them, and also distributed them by yourself. You cared very little for your personal convenience. Your only thought was to satisfy your spiritual master and give us Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are inspired by your dedication and determination. By your example you showed us how to overcome all obstacles and become successful. Such success is possible only by following the instructions of the spiritual master.

Due to your efforts, everything is now easy for us. We have temples, your books, the holy names, and your wonderful disciples, who are carrying on your mission. These disciples are like bright moons illuminating this planet with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Expertly trained by you, they are enlivening all the devotees. We are so fortunate to have their association and guidance in our spiritual lives. This is part of the legacy you left us—the many wonderful devotees who are inspiring us in your physical absence and carrying on your mission.

You also established for us the proper conclusion of spiritual life. In a poem to your spiritual master you wrote, “Absolute is sentient, thou hast proved/ Impersonal calamity thou hast removed.” You showed us that the Absolute is a person, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and that we can have eternal loving pastimes with Him. You dispelled all our illusions about real spiritual life.

By your mercy we can achieve that goal. On your arrival in America you wrote a beautiful prayer to the Lord, entitled “Preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in America.” In that prayer you humbly prayed to the Lord for His mercy, for the ability to spread His message. You fully depended on the Lord for success in your mission. You wrote, “I wish that You may deliver them.” By your will this is happening, since Kṛṣṇa is obliged to His pure devotee.

You were able to import the entire Vaiṣṇava culture to the West, without change, including exclusive devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa. No one thought this could be done. Your godbrothers couldn’t understand how Lord Caitanya’s prediction that His name would be chanted in every town and village would be fulfilled. But you believed it, and you were empowered to fulfill it.

You requested us to take what you gave us, become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and then give it to others. We pray to you that we may fully embrace your teachings and instructions. As you struggled for our welfare, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we enthusiastically work for the welfare of others. We pray that our only motive will be to obtain pure devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa by your mercy, and to give it to others.

*Your* aspiring servants at ISKCON Phoenix, Arizona, USA.  
(written by Cakrī Dāsa)

## Phuket

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Our center, located near the beach in Phuket, Thailand, was begun on Rāma-navamī 2007. At our recent fourth-anniversary celebration, in addition to celebrating Lord Rāma’s appearance we held our first Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā. In attendance were many exalted devotees, including our GBC, His Holiness Kavicandra Mahārāja; our co-GBC, His Holiness Bhakti-vighna-vināśa Narasiṁha Mahārāja; His Holiness Vedavyāsa-priya Mahārāja; and many international devotees. We hope we can hold bigger programs in the near feature. We beg to become eternal servants of your divine lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so we can progress in our spiritual life and help others do so as well.

Your servants at ISKCON of Phuket, Thailand.  
(written by Ravikula Rāma Dāsa)

## Port Elizabeth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

First of all we would like to thank you very much for giving us your wonderful sons, who are selflessly working very hard to develop your preaching mission, and who are always there to pull us out of *māyā* and engage us in loving devotional service. On the Internet we are able to see and hear how His Holiness Jayapatāka Swami, despite serious health concerns, is continuing to travel and preach extensively. We realize that all this is possible only by your causeless mercy. His Holiness Girirāj Swami (my Guru Mahārāja), His Holiness Bhakti Caitanya Swami, His Holiness Pārthasarathi Mahārāja, His Holiness Gopāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami, His Holiness Rādhānath Swami and all your other sons and daughters whom you left to guide us—all are very valuable to us. Without them we would all be helpless in our efforts to progress in devotional service. We would have lots of impediments and would end up like stray dogs on the street, with no master or proper shelter. We would catch all kinds of diseases, especially the most pernicious one of always blaspheming swanlike devotees instead of glorifying them. Śrīla Prabhupāda, out of your oceanic compassion you have given us so many facilities for us to come out of our suffering condition. Yet many of us end up going back to our miserable lives. It’s amazing.

On this very special day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we all beg you to please keep our spiritual masters, your beloved sons, very healthy. Please continue to give them and all of us your blessings and make us all very strong. We will never be able to thank you enough for giving us the golden opportunity to taste the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in our lifetimes.

The servants of your servants at ISKCON of Port Elizabeth, South Africa.  
(written by Śvetadvīpa Dāsa)

## Puerto Rico

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, our master and guide.

We were all drying up for want of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and there you appeared, like an oasis in a desert.

Here in Puerto Rico we are all trying to please you in different ways. Even though we are a small temple, we are accomplishing something. The temple now owns a restaurant, where we daily distribute *prasādam* and preach. It is situated in a populated area and is doing well.

The Deities’ program has been maintained so far, and the agriculture program has also been going on. The temple is now growing such foods as plantains, pumpkins, cilantro, and zucchini. We beg you to accept these humble offerings of ours. Even though we are so few and insignificant, we are trying to do something to please you. Our love for you is shown in substantive ways. Similarly, your love for your Guru Mahārāja took the form of a gigantic worldwide society of Vaiṣṇavas—ISKCON. We pray to become instruments in the service of your mission.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your books, because without them we could not make it in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. They are essential both for our own hearing and chanting and for distributing to the conditioned souls, who do not know anything about Kṛṣṇa. Just recalling how pleased you would become by hearing the book scores is enough for us to keep passing them out. With the rise in oil prices, the economy is getting worse, so we use this opportunity to distribute more books. The people’s suffering is increasing, and so is book distribution worldwide. We get the most pleasure by distributing your books to the ignorant people of this Age of Kali. It is not a difficult task, and we are very thankful to you for giving us this service. You said, “Distribute books! Distribute books! Distribute books!” We have made this our life, and we know this is the only way for us to please you. Every day we meet people who have one or more of your books, and we can see how their lives are being changed. More and more souls are reading your books and coming in contact with your movement. Your great work is going on unendingly. You are constantly at work delivering the fallen souls. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please engage us more and more in your service, as you see fit. We at ISKCON Puerto Rico are helplessly trying to push on your mission of spreading Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. Just like the little bird who was trying to dry up the ocean with its beak, we feel very tiny and helpless in the face of this gigantic task of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Kali-yuga.

Your fallen servants in Puerto Rico.  
(written by Navanīta Taskara Dāsa)

## Radhadesh

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We residents of Radhadesh beg to offer our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

Although you never personally visited Radhadesh, we are daily blessed to be happy and grateful that our Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā Devī were personally installed by Your Divine Grace. Not only that, but in a letter to Locanānanda Prabhu of 5 January 1976, you mercifully wrote, “Your Deities shall be named Rādhā-Gopīnātha.” Thus when we take *darśana* of our *iṣṭa-deva*, we recall that without your causeless compassion we would never have had such a vision of the Lord. In fact, practicing Vaiṣṇavism would have been inconceivable for us. Further on in that same letter, you wrote

Upon your recommendation I am pleased to accept as my initiated disciples: First initiation; Mr. Claude Nowe—Klesapaha-dasa, Bert de Vos—Vrsnivamsa-dasa, Belgin Kaya—Vedasmrit-dasi, and Maria Eugenia Jorquera—Marutvati-dasi; Second initiation; Jaya Gopala dasa, Janaraja dasa, Aharadi dasa.

Three of these souls have passed away since then, and we are fully confident that because of your merciful intervention in their lives, and because of their decades of devoted service in your mission, their destinations can be nothing other than auspicious. You were always so grateful to your disciples for their giving their youthful energy and lives to you, and they remain eternally indebted for your endless sacrifice to bring them to the Lord’s devotional service. Furthermore, it was Your Divine Grace who advised those early followers not to rent the castle and property now named Radhadesh, but to wait until it was to be sold. Because your servants followed that directive, not only are we now sheltered in this *yātrā,* but it is also a haven for many devotees who come here from around the world to attend Bhaktivedanta College and to participate in the many transcendental retreats and festivals held here several times a year. Not only does Radhadesh serve as a transcendental home and shelter for a thriving community of devotees, but it also hosts thousands of visitors yearly. All these visitors have the opportunity to take *darśana* of Their Lordships, chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, receive *prasādam,* and learn of your voyage across the Atlantic while advanced in age and frail in body and your miraculous feats in establishing the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement worldwide. For devotees preaching all over the Benelux, it is not unusual to meet people who are familiar with Radhadesh, having visited the *yātrā* repeatedly because of its friendly and peaceful atmosphere.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we who are privileged to serve in your Radhadesh *yātrā* are, of course, aware of our weaknesses and the challenges we face in carrying on your mission here. We pray that by your continuing blessings upon us we may not take for granted our being allowed to serve in this holy place, and that we may overcome our shortcomings in your service by working cooperatively, as you desired, to always guard and improve the standard of our offering to you and Their Lordships. Kindly continue to glance favorably upon us so that we may remain enthusiastic and useful in your service, and so that we may also extend the opportunity for others to do so, for your satisfaction.

Your servants at Radhadesh, Belgium.

## Richmond Hill

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this day so auspicious  
We desire to reflect on what you mean to us.  
Without you, where would we be?  
In the clutches of Māyā, most certainly.  
We were wandering around clueless,  
Pursuing nescience in this age of darkness.  
You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the only reason  
That we have a chance to get out of Māyās prison.  
You took your Guru Mahārāja’s instruction  
And followed through with unlimited success and precision.  
You worked constantly day and night  
To save us from our plight.  
*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is*—  
Without deviation or misrepresentation, you gave us all this.  
You showed us the path to true happiness,  
The path we must pursue in order to achieve success.  
What you have accomplished is nothing short of miraculous.  
The gift of bhakti-yoga so precious.  
Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura foretold of an ācārya so great.  
For you to come in our lifetime, we are so fortunate.  
Lord Caitanya predicted that the holy names would spread in every town and village,  
And you came and accomplished that with boldness and courage.  
You gave us the genuine treasures,  
Certainly worth immensely more than gems, jewels, and material pleasures.  
You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are our savior;  
You exemplified perfect Vaiṣṇava behavior.  
You gave us hundreds of temples worldwide,  
Giving us Lord Kṛṣṇa personified.  
You wrote scores and scores of books,  
Encompassing everything we need if we’d only look.  
You explained everything in thousands of lectures,  
Cared for us like a father with your loving gestures.  
Your lotus feet traveled around the globe fourteen times,  
Opening our hearts and minds to Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes.  
You started the Back to Godhead magazine,  
Giving everyone the opportunity to see what life should mean.  
You are perfect in every way—you are bona fide.  
Your instructions we wish to follow and abide.  
You wrote personal letters in the thousands,  
Your words more valuable than diamonds.  
You accurately answered every doubt and question,  
Explaining and answering with purity and perfection.  
We humbly wish to follow your request;  
Although we are not qualified we want to try our best.  
You wanted to take us back home, back to Godhead,  
And gave us all the tools to put us in good stead.  
Kṛṣṇa consciousness has been and will be exposed to generations upon generations.  
Your vāṇī continue through your books, lectures, and instructions.  
You provided us with the true meaning of life,  
Saving us from material misery and strife.  
You are our spiritual master;  
We bow down at your feet forever.  
You are our guru;  
We truly are among the lucky few.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, by Your Divine Grace  
We have been given this nectar so great.  
Actually, it would take too many words to describe what you mean to us.  
We hope this humble attempt will be accepted by you—so gracious.  
Thank you for having mercy upon us fallen souls.  
Thank you for giving us the highest priorities and goals.

Please allow us to continue to serve you and Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and Kṛṣṇa.  
Please help us continue in the realm of the beat of the ISKCON mṛdaṅga.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Gurudeva!

Your humble servants in Richmond Hill, Canada.  
(written by Mīnākṣī Devī Dāsī)

## Rome

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I offer my respectful obeisance to you, the spiritual master of the whole universe.

You have given us access to the spiritual mission of Śrī *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu, and you have given us the opportunity to participate in the intimate, transcendental pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, whose pastimes in the guise of a compassionate devotee are flooding the whole universe with love and devotion to God.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s state of mind, day and night, was practically identical to Rādhārāṇīs state of mind when Uddhava came to Vṛndāvana to see the *gopīs*. The Lord constantly exhibited a state of mind reflecting the madness of separation. Blood flowed from all the pores of His body, and all His teeth were loosened. At one moment His whole body became slender, and at another moment His whole body became fat. The joints of His hands and legs would sometimes become separated by eight inches, and they remained connected only by the skin. Sometimes Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s hands, legs, and head would all enter within His body, just like the withdrawn limbs of a tortoise. In this way Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu used to manifest wonderful ecstatic symptoms. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu used to express His mind in this way: “Where is the Lord of My life, who is playing His flute? What shall I do now? Where should I go to find the son of Mahārāja Nanda? To whom should I speak? Who can understand My disappointment? Without the son of Nanda Mahārāja, My heart is broken.” \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 2.4–6, 12–16\]

Such incomparable ecstatic feelings are seen only in the Lord and in the hearts of His intimate associates, His pure devotees.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, I do not know Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī*, or Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, but I’m able to perceive your boundless compassion for all the conditioned souls of this material world and your extraordinary ability to offer anyone who desires it the chance to go back to Kṛṣṇa in the spiritual world.

I desire to feel those same ecstatic symptoms of separation toward you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Sometimes I wonder, “Where are you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, spiritual master of my life? Where are you now playing your *saṅkīrtana’s* ‘big *mṛdaṅga*’? How can I serve you constantly and without personal material desires? Separated from you, my heart is broken.”

Under the incessant flow of time, for an instant these thoughts arise in my mind, just to unfortunately disappear the next moment, giving way to useless material thoughts.

At times, just for a blessed moment my understanding may capture the profound significance of the words in your wonderful books, but then some pressing material necessity easily distracts my mind from the spiritual objective that you have kindly donated to us.

When I think of your spiritual greatness, I realize my complete disqualification, but whenever I obtain the mercy to do something that pleases you, I get permeated by the strong feeling of being empowered to overcome any hurdle. With you near us, by your causeless mercy all difficulties are shattered and it becomes possible to accomplish any goal.

Every day, while trying, as a humble offering to Your Divine Grace, to develop the project here in Rome by spreading your absolute spiritual vision, we persistently experience the feeling of being incapable of eventually attaining success. But as soon as we simply try to apply your instructions according to the same priorities and modality that Your Divine Grace established, everything immediately becomes feasible.

The hindrances are due only to our seemingly feeble confidence in your teachings, teachings that have already surpassed the test of time and are by themselves the definitive means to overcome every obstacle.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if we do not feel separation from you, deeply appreciating your pure unalloyed devotion to your great spiritual master and to the Lord, then we are still spiritually sick and must work hard to re-establish our normal condition and at least be considered human beings.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, your divine lotus feet have walked the streets of this city, and as a result Rome, the so-called “eternal city,” has been transformed forever.

We are simultaneously enthused and very curious to know how you will engage us in devotional service in this place that is at the at once so degraded but, because of your presence here, so fascinating and mysterious.

Your Divine Grace’s aspiring eternal servants in Rome, Italy.  
(written by Līlānanda Dāsa)

## Sacramento

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you.

We were born in the darkness of ignorance, and you opened our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. Therefore we fall at your feet again and again.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if we had not met Your Divine Grace, we would have been swallowed by all-devouring Time and fallen into the endless caverns of hell, all the while thinking ourselves successful people in this material world. Until we came into contact with Your Divine Grace, we never knew that this world is a place of endless birth and death or that we should use our intelligence to try to find a permanent way out of this dangerous situation.

Along with this secret knowledge of transcendental existence, we are experiencing a higher taste of happiness that washes away all material cares and gives us a new vision of the purpose of life. Also, it amazes us to know that real knowledge of the position of Lord Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Enjoyer and Supersoul situated in the heart of all living entities was lying hidden for so many generations in five-thousand-year-old texts, the true import of which was known to only a few *sādhus* and *bābājīs,* but which are now speaking volumes of transcendental instructions to us, the children of a technologically advanced post-industrial society. That is the miracle of your translations of Śrīla Vyāsadeva’s sacred texts, such as the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Now we are able to go through these books with relish and find that every word, originally written on ancient palm-leaves, is urgently relevant to us today.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have brought this ancient knowledge from obscurity to the light of day and thus given all the fallen souls of Kali-yuga the opportunity to get out of their horrible predicament.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, merciful savior of the three worlds, we are eternally indebted to you. Please accept us into your eternal association.

Your humble servants at ISKCON of Sacramento, California, USA.

## San Antonio

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, this is the first time we devotees in San Antonio have written an homage for your Vyāsa-pūjā book, although Lord Caitanya’s mission has been going here since 1972, when your dear devotee His Holiness Viṣṇujana Swami started a center here.

Today, by the mercy of another dear devotee of yours, His Holiness Guru Prasāda Swami, we are honored to continue your legacy here as the ISKCON Mission Society.

Historically, San Antonio is the site of many centuries-old Christian missions and pioneer churches, whose remains are still visible. The only way to keep the spirit of these missions going today is by what you gave us—Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Otherwise, nothing remains but ruins.

*pāhi pāhi mahā-yogin deva-deva jagat-pate*  
*nānyaṁ tvad abhayaṁ paśye yatra mṛtyuḥ parasparam*

**“\[Uttarā said:\] O Lord of lords, Lord of the universe! You are the greatest of mystics. Please protect me, protect me, for there is no one else who can save me from the clutches of death in this world of duality.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*****. 1.8.9)**

For several years now, your mission here in San Antonio—the ISKCON Mission Society—has been attracting more and more local youth, especially artists and musicians, all very talented and sincere souls. On this most auspicious day of you divine appearance, we pray to your lotus feet that you see to the spiritual welfare of these sincere souls. Please guide them and provide them with whatever is necessary so that they can please Kṛṣṇa and return to Him.

As we take these pioneering initiatives, we are certain you are attentive and very interested to see your mission here flourish. We pray to the holy name of the lord and to the most merciful Pañca-tattva for the strength to be an instrument in your hands as you lead us to newer and newer frontiers of your (still) pioneering movement.

Always depending on your mercy, we remain  
Your servants in San Antonio, Texas, USA.

## Scarborough

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance.

Even though we’re unqualified, we beg that you accept this small offering and favor us with your mercy.

One more year has passed in separation from you, but still you’re with us, every day, in your divine instructions. Just as a lighthouse warns passing ships of dangerous shoals, so your divine instructions continue to provide direction for many misdirected living entities.

Here in your Scarborough temple, we’re trying hard to follow your instructions by engaging in preaching activities and conducting our temple programs. We’ve expanded our preaching activities outside the temple by conducting regular home programs. Every evening during the month of Dāmodara we visited a different home and conducted *kīrtana* and a short class. All the householders and their friends and families got an opportunity to offer ghee lamps to Lord Dāmodara, many for the first time.

Last year we organized a unique *saṅkīrtana* cruise aboard a ship on Lake Ontario. The program introduced many people to the holy name for the first time. Some are now showing keen interest in taking up Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We’ve increased our preaching by other means too, such as person-to-person contact, radio, and the Internet.

Over the past year we were fortunate to receive the association of several senior devotees, who are helping to keep us in touch with you and your instructions.

We have a strong team of devotees who serve your center in various ways, such as cooking, Deity worship, and book distribution.

In closing, we pray for your mercy so we can humbly engage in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. With sincerity we beg for your blessings to continue to serve you as we attempt to fulfill the purpose of your mission.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Scarborough, Ontario, Canada.

## Srinagar

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your divine personality.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the savior of the whole world because you are preaching *sanātana*-*dharma* (Kṛṣṇa consciousness) throughout the whole world. You have fulfilled the desire of Lord *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*

**“In every town and village of the world, the chanting of My name will be heard.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are now fulfilling Lord Caitanya’s prediction even in this part of the world (Kashmir), which had heretofore been left untouched. For the last five years we have been trying to establish ISKCON under the guidance of your sincere disciple His Holiness Nava Yogendra Swami. It is very difficult, since Vedic culture is almost invisible here because of the demoniac atmosphere.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said that if one preaches in a Muslim land “I will take the dust of his feet on my head.” On this day of your appearance we beg you to please give us the strength to help establish a pure Kṛṣṇa conscious center here for your pleasure and that of *Śrī* Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. This, you also said, is the perfection of the human form of life.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your ISKCON is Vaikuṇṭha in this material world. One can experience that by living in an ISKCON center where your instructions are being followed sincerely and strictly. We see Kali-yuga spreading its influence everywhere, but your divine literature will push back the influence of Kali and guide humanity for the coming ten thousand years. Please give us the strength to distribute your divine literature, which can save humanity from going to the dogs. Anyone can experience your presence in your divine literature and, by following your teachings in your books, can ultimately realize his relationship with the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this land of Kashmir was once considered the highest seat of learning. But nowadays it is the highest seat of Kali-yuga—filled with quarrel and hypocrisy. It will only get worse, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unless you mercifully empower us to spread far and wide your divine literature and the holy name of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa to the inhabitants of this tract of land. Please bless us in this way.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

All glories to ISKCON!

Your servants at ISKCON Srinagar, Kashmir.  
(written by Sudarśana Dāsa)

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on this glorious day.

Once Tamāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami had a dream in which he saw you give a preaching report to the *guru*-*paramparā*. In that report you said, “My disciples are not very knowledgeable, not very renounced, and also not very cultured. But they have one good qualification: they are very committed to serving me.”

When Tamāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami related this dream to you, you confirmed that the dream was a true and accurate description of reality. Although we may claim that the qualifications of your followers have improved since the 1970s, this does not change the fact that we are solely a product of your mercy and therefore that our attachment to you and our commitment to your service are our main assets, even today.

As you yourself said, you gave us the qualification to serve in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* mission. You allowed us and invited us to serve. Moreover, as a pure representative of Lord *Nityānanda*, you tirelessly traveled all over the world to canvass everyone to worship Lord *Gaurāṅga* by chanting the holy names. And you continue to do the same now through your books and the services of your followers.

Thinking of your life, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we find the lines from the famous verse recited by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu ringing in our ears: *gopī-bhartuḥ pada-kamalayor dāsa-dāsānudāsaḥ*. You demonstrated by your own life of service to Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura that the position of being a servant of the servant of *Śrī* Kṛṣṇa is the perfection of life and the eternal position of the soul.

By so kindly accepting our service, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have directly placed us in our eternal position as a spirit soul. There is nothing more to ask for, there is nothing more to pray for.
